/*
  Nombre del archivo: App.jsx
  PropÃ³sito: Componente principal de RendixApp con layout unificado, control de sesiÃ³n,
  rendimiento manual por vehÃ­culo y sincronizaciÃ³n con Supabase + API CNE.
*/

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import './App.css';
import { supabase } from './supabaseClient';
import { fetchRealCnePrices } from './services/cneService';
import {
  calculateFleetAverageEfficiency,
  getManualEfficiency
} from './utils/efficiencyHelper';

import Auth from './components/Auth';
import Sidebar from './components/Sidebar';
import Toast from './components/Toast';

import DashboardView from './views/DashboardView';
import VehiclesView from './views/VehiclesView';
import FuelView from './views/FuelView';
import ExpensesView from './views/ExpensesView';
import CneView from './views/CneView';
import AlertsView from './views/AlertsView';
import DocumentsView from './views/DocumentsView'; 
import TripView from './views/TripView';

import VehicleModal from './modals/VehicleModal';
import FuelModal from './modals/FuelModal';
import ExpenseModal from './modals/ExpenseModal';
import ServiceModal from './modals/ServiceModal';
import AnalyticsModal from './modals/AnalyticsModal';

export default function App() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('dashboard');
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState([]);
  
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState('dark');
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const [modalState, setModalState] = useState({ type: null, data: null });
  const [isCneLoading, setIsCneLoading] = useState(false);

  const [vehicles, setVehicles] = useState([]);
  const [fuelRecords, setFuelRecords] = useState([]);
  const [expenses, setExpenses] = useState([]);
  const [cneStations, setCneStations] = useState([]);

  const [dashVehicleFilter, setDashVehicleFilter] = useState('all');

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme);
  }, [theme]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    setTheme(prev => (prev === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark'));
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data: { session } }) => {
      setSession(session);
    });

    const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {
      setSession(session);
    });

    return () => subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (session) {
      fetchUserVehicles();
      fetchFuelRecords();
      fetchExpenses();
    }
  }, [session]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      session &&
      (activeTab === 'cne' || activeTab === 'fuel') &&
      cneStations.length === 0 &&
      !isCneLoading
    ) {
      fetchCneStations(false);
    }
  }, [session, activeTab]);

  const fetchUserVehicles = async () => {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
      .from('user_vehicles')
      .select('*')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

    if (!error && data) {
      const formatted = data.map(v => ({
        id: v.id,
        name: v.custom_name,
        brand: v.brand || 'Sin marca',
        model: v.model || 'Sin modelo',
        year: v.year || new Date().getFullYear(),
        color: v.color || '#4f46e5',
        currentKm: Number(v.current_km || 0),
        plate: v.plate || 'SIN PATENTE',
        image_url: v.image_url || '',
        manual_efficiency: v.manual_efficiency ? Number(v.manual_efficiency) : '',
        last_service_km: Number(v.last_service_km || 0),
        last_service_date: v.last_service_date || '',
        
        soap_expiration: v.soap_expiration || '',
        permiso_circulacion_expiration: v.permiso_circulacion_expiration || '',
        revision_tecnica_expiration: v.revision_tecnica_expiration || '',
        seguro_privado_expiration: v.seguro_privado_expiration || '',
        licencia_conducir_expiration: v.licencia_conducir_expiration || '',
        
        soap_file: v.soap_file || '',
        permiso_file: v.permiso_file || '',
        revision_file: v.revision_file || '',
        seguro_file: v.seguro_file || '',
        licencia_file: v.licencia_file || ''
      }));
      setVehicles(formatted);
    } else if (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching vehicles:', error);
    }
  };

  const fetchFuelRecords = async () => {
    const { data, error } = await supabase.from('fuel_records').select('*').order('date', { ascending: false });
    if (!error && data) setFuelRecords(data);
  };

  const fetchExpenses = async () => {
    const { data, error } = await supabase.from('expenses').select('*').order('date', { ascending: false });
    if (!error && data) setExpenses(data);
  };

  const fetchCneStations = async (forceRetry = false) => {
    setIsCneLoading(true);
    try {
      if (cneStations.length === 0) {
        const { data, error } = await supabase
          .from('cne_stations')
          .select('*')
          .order('commune', { ascending: true });

        if (!error && data && data.length > 0) {
          setCneStations(data);
        }
      }

      const liveStations = await fetchRealCnePrices({
        regionFilter: 'Metropolitana',
        forceRetry
      });

      if (liveStations && liveStations.length > 0) {
        setCneStations(liveStations);
        if (forceRetry) showToast('âœ… Precios actualizados desde API CNE');
      } else if (forceRetry) {
        showToast('âš ï¸ API CNE temporalmente saturada; mostrando respaldo en nube');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error al obtener estaciones CNE:', err);
    } finally {
      setIsCneLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const showToast = (message) => {
    const id = Date.now();
    setToasts(prev => [...prev, { id, message }]);
    setTimeout(() => setToasts(prev => prev.filter(t => t.id !== id)), 3500);
  };

  const closeModal = () => setModalState({ type: null, data: null });
  const openModal = (type, data = null) => setModalState({ type, data });

  const handleVehicleSubmit = async (form) => {
    if (!session) return;
    const payload = {
      user_id: session.user.id,
      global_vehicle_id: form.global_vehicle_id || null,
      custom_name: form.name,
      brand: form.brand,
      model: form.model,
      year: Number(form.year),
      current_km: Number(form.currentKm),
      plate: form.plate ? form.plate.trim().toUpperCase() : 'SIN PATENTE',
      image_url: form.image_url || null,
      manual_efficiency:
        form.manual_efficiency && Number(form.manual_efficiency) > 0
          ? Number(form.manual_efficiency)
          : null
    };
    if (form.id) {
      await supabase.from('user_vehicles').update(payload).eq('id', form.id);
      showToast('âœ¨ VehÃ­culo actualizado');
    } else {
      await supabase.from('user_vehicles').insert([payload]);
      showToast('ðŸŽ‰ VehÃ­culo registrado');
    }
    fetchUserVehicles();
    closeModal();
  };

  const handleUpdateManualEfficiency = async (vehicleId, manualEfficiency) => {
    if (!session) return;
    const val = manualEfficiency && Number(manualEfficiency) > 0 ? Number(manualEfficiency) : null;
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('user_vehicles')
      .update({ manual_efficiency: val })
      .eq('id', vehicleId);

    if (!error) {
      setVehicles(prev =>
        prev.map(v => (v.id === vehicleId ? { ...v, manual_efficiency: val || '' } : v))
      );
      showToast('âœ… Rendimiento actualizado');
    } else {
      console.error('Error al actualizar rendimiento manual:', error);
      showToast('âŒ No se pudo guardar el rendimiento');
    }
  };

  const deleteVehicle = async (id) => {
    if (window.confirm('Â¿EstÃ¡s seguro de eliminar este vehÃ­culo?')) {
      await supabase.from('user_vehicles').delete().eq('id', id);
      fetchUserVehicles();
      fetchFuelRecords();
      fetchExpenses();
      showToast('ðŸ—‘ï¸ VehÃ­culo eliminado');
    }
  };

  const handleFuelSubmit = async (form) => {
    if (!session) return;
    const odoVal = form.odometer && Number(form.odometer) > 0 ? Number(form.odometer) : null;

    const payload = {
      user_id: session.user.id,
      vehicle_id: form.vehicle_id || form.vehicleId,
      date: form.date || new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
      liters: Number(form.liters || 0),
      total: Number(form.total || 0),
      odometer: odoVal,
      fuel_type: form.fuel_type || '95',
      station: form.station || '',
      is_full_tank: true
    };
    const { data } = await supabase.from('fuel_records').insert([payload]).select();
    if (data) setFuelRecords(prev => [data[0], ...prev]);
    showToast('â›½ Carga registrada');
    closeModal();
  };

  const handleDeleteFuel = async (id) => {
    if (window.confirm('Â¿Eliminar carga?')) {
      await supabase.from('fuel_records').delete().eq('id', id);
      setFuelRecords(prev => prev.filter(item => item.id !== id));
      showToast('ðŸ—‘ï¸ Carga eliminada');
    }
  };

  const handleExpenseSubmit = async (form) => {
    if (!session) return;
    const payload = {
      user_id: session.user.id,
      vehicle_id: form.vehicle_id || form.vehicleId,
      date: form.date || new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
      amount: Number(form.amount || 0),
      category: form.category || 'Otros',
      description: form.description || ''
    };
    const { data } = await supabase.from('expenses').insert([payload]).select();
    if (data) setExpenses(prev => [data[0], ...prev]);
    showToast('ðŸ’° Gasto registrado');
    closeModal();
  };

  const handleDeleteExpense = async (id) => {
    if (window.confirm('Â¿Eliminar gasto?')) {
      await supabase.from('expenses').delete().eq('id', id);
      setExpenses(prev => prev.filter(item => item.id !== id));
      showToast('ðŸ—‘ï¸ Gasto eliminado');
    }
  };

  const handleServiceSubmit = async (form) => {
    if (!session) return;
    await supabase.from('user_vehicles').update({
      last_service_date: form.last_service_date,
      last_service_km: form.last_service_km
    }).eq('id', form.vehicleId);
    fetchUserVehicles();
    closeModal();
    showToast('ðŸ”§ MantenciÃ³n registrada');
  };

  const handleSaveDocuments = async (vehicleId, updatedDocs) => {
    if (!session) return;
    
    try {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('user_vehicles')
        .update(updatedDocs)
        .eq('id', vehicleId);
      
      if (error) {
        console.error('Error al guardar documentos en Supabase:', error);
        alert(`âŒ Error al guardar: ${error.message}`);
      } else {
        await fetchUserVehicles();
        showToast('âœ¨ Billetera digital guardada en la nube');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error al guardar Billetera:', err);
    }
  };

  const getAlertCount = () => {
    let count = 0;
    vehicles.forEach(v => {
      const currentKm = Number(v.currentKm || 0);
      const lastServiceKm = Number(v.last_service_km || 0);
      const intervalKm = 10000;
      const nextServiceKm = lastServiceKm > 0 ? lastServiceKm + intervalKm : Math.ceil((currentKm + 1) / intervalKm) * intervalKm;
      const remainingKm = nextServiceKm - currentKm;

      if (lastServiceKm > 0 && remainingKm <= 1500) count++;
      const docs = [v.soap_expiration, v.permiso_circulacion_expiration, v.revision_tecnica_expiration, v.seguro_privado_expiration, v.licencia_conducir_expiration];
      docs.forEach(dateStr => {
        if (dateStr && Math.ceil((new Date(dateStr) - new Date()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) <= 30) count++;
      });
    });
    return count;
  };

  const filteredVehicles = dashVehicleFilter === 'all' ? vehicles : vehicles.filter(v => v.id === dashVehicleFilter);
  const filteredFuels = dashVehicleFilter === 'all' ? fuelRecords : fuelRecords.filter(f => (f.vehicle_id || f.vehicleId) === dashVehicleFilter);
  const filteredExpenses = dashVehicleFilter === 'all' ? expenses : expenses.filter(e => (e.vehicle_id || e.vehicleId) === dashVehicleFilter);
  const totalFuelCost = filteredFuels.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + Number(curr.total || 0), 0);
  const totalExpenses = filteredExpenses.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + Number(curr.amount || 0), 0);
  const totalCargas = filteredFuels.length;

  const avgKm = calculateFleetAverageEfficiency(filteredVehicles);

  const calculateCostPerKm = () => {
    if (!filteredFuels || filteredFuels.length === 0) return '0';

    // 1. Estimar kilÃ³metros recorridos usando (litros cargados * rendimiento manual km/L)
    let estimatedKm = 0;
    filteredFuels.forEach(r => {
      const vId = r.vehicle_id || r.vehicleId;
      const vehicle = vehicles.find(v => v.id === vId);
      const manualKmL = getManualEfficiency(vehicle);
      const liters = Number(r.liters || 0);
      if (manualKmL > 0 && liters > 0) {
        estimatedKm += liters * manualKmL;
      }
    });

    // 2. Respaldo por diferencia de odÃ³metro si no hay rendimiento manual configurado
    if (estimatedKm === 0) {
      const recordsByVehicle = {};
      filteredFuels.forEach(r => {
        const vId = r.vehicle_id || r.vehicleId;
        const odo = Number(r.odometer || 0);
        if (!vId || odo <= 0) return;
        if (!recordsByVehicle[vId]) recordsByVehicle[vId] = [];
        recordsByVehicle[vId].push(r);
      });

      Object.values(recordsByVehicle).forEach(vRecords => {
        if (vRecords.length < 2) return;
        const sorted = [...vRecords].sort((a, b) => Number(a.odometer) - Number(b.odometer));
        const kmDiff = Number(sorted[sorted.length - 1].odometer) - Number(sorted[0].odometer);
        if (kmDiff > 0) estimatedKm += kmDiff;
      });
    }

    if (estimatedKm <= 0) return '0';
    const grandTotalCost = totalFuelCost + totalExpenses;
    return Math.round(grandTotalCost / estimatedKm).toLocaleString('es-CL');
  };

  if (!session) {
    return <Auth onLoginSuccess={(sess) => { setSession(sess); showToast('ðŸ” Â¡Bienvenido!'); }} />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-container">
      <Toast toasts={toasts} />

      <div className="mobile-topbar">
        <button 
          onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(prev => !prev)} 
          style={{ background: 'transparent', border: 'none', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '22px', cursor: 'pointer' }}
        >
          â˜°
        </button>
        <div style={{ fontWeight: '900', fontSize: '16px', color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>
          Rendix<span style={{ color: '#38bdf8' }}>App</span>
        </div>
        <button onClick={toggleTheme} style={{ background: 'transparent', border: 'none', fontSize: '18px', cursor: 'pointer' }}>
          {theme === 'dark' ? 'ðŸŒ™' : 'â˜€ï¸'}
        </button>
      </div>
      
      <Sidebar 
        activeTab={activeTab} 
        setActiveTab={setActiveTab} 
        alertCount={getAlertCount()} 
        theme={theme}
        toggleTheme={toggleTheme}
        isOpen={mobileMenuOpen}
        onCloseMobile={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}
      />

      <main className="main-content">
        {activeTab === 'dashboard' && (
          <DashboardView 
            vehicles={vehicles}
            filteredVehicles={filteredVehicles}
            filteredFuels={filteredFuels}
            filteredExpenses={filteredExpenses}
            totalFuelCost={totalFuelCost}
            totalExpenses={totalExpenses}
            totalCargas={totalCargas}
            avgKm={avgKm}
            costPerKm={calculateCostPerKm()}
            dashVehicleFilter={dashVehicleFilter}
            setDashVehicleFilter={setDashVehicleFilter}
            setActiveTab={setActiveTab}
            openAnalyticsModal={() => openModal('analytics')}
          />
        )}
        {activeTab === 'vehicles' && (
          <VehiclesView
            vehicles={vehicles}
            deleteVehicle={deleteVehicle}
            openModal={(v) => openModal('vehicle', v)}
            onUpdateManualEfficiency={handleUpdateManualEfficiency}
          />
        )}
        {activeTab === 'documents' && <DocumentsView vehicles={vehicles} onSaveDocuments={handleSaveDocuments} />}
        {activeTab === 'trip' && <TripView vehicles={vehicles} />}
        {activeTab === 'fuel' && (
          <FuelView
            fuelRecords={fuelRecords}
            vehicles={vehicles}
            cneStations={cneStations}
            isCneLoading={isCneLoading}
            openModal={() => openModal('fuel')}
            onDeleteFuel={handleDeleteFuel}
          />
        )}
        {activeTab === 'expenses' && <ExpensesView expenses={expenses} vehicles={vehicles} openModal={() => openModal('expense')} onDeleteExpense={handleDeleteExpense} />}
        {activeTab === 'cne' && (
          <CneView
            cneStations={cneStations}
            isLoading={isCneLoading}
            onReload={() => fetchCneStations(true)}
          />
        )}
        {activeTab === 'alerts' && <AlertsView vehicles={vehicles} openServiceModal={(vehicle) => openModal('service', vehicle)} />}
      </main>

      {modalState.type === 'vehicle' && <VehicleModal editingVehicle={modalState.data} onClose={closeModal} onSubmit={handleVehicleSubmit} />}
      {modalState.type === 'fuel' && <FuelModal vehicles={vehicles} onClose={closeModal} onSubmit={handleFuelSubmit} />}
      {modalState.type === 'expense' && <ExpenseModal vehicles={vehicles} onClose={closeModal} onSubmit={handleExpenseSubmit} />}
      {modalState.type === 'service' && <ServiceModal vehicle={modalState.data} onClose={closeModal} onSubmit={handleServiceSubmit} />}
      {modalState.type === 'analytics' && <AnalyticsModal fuelRecords={fuelRecords} expenses={expenses} onClose={closeModal} />}
    </div>
  );
}

==================== ARCHIVO: src\components\Auth.jsx ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: Auth.jsx
  PropÃ³sito: Pantalla de inicio de sesiÃ³n y registro de usuarios con Supabase Auth.
*/

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '../supabaseClient';

export default function Auth({ onLoginSuccess }) {
  const [isSignUp, setIsSignUp] = useState(false);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState({ text: '', type: '' });

  const handleAuth = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setMessage({ text: '', type: '' });

    try {
      if (isSignUp) {
        // Registrar nuevo usuario
        const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
          email,
          password
        });

        if (error) throw error;

        setMessage({
          text: 'Â¡Registro exitoso! Revisa tu correo o inicia sesiÃ³n.',
          type: 'success'
        });
        setIsSignUp(false);
      } else {
        // Iniciar sesiÃ³n
        const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
          email,
          password
        });

        if (error) throw error;

        if (data.session) {
          onLoginSuccess(data.session);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setMessage({ text: err.message || 'Error en la autenticaciÃ³n', type: 'error' });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div style={{
      minHeight: '100vh',
      display: 'flex',
      alignItems: 'center',
      justifyContent: 'center',
      backgroundColor: '#0f172a',
      color: '#f8fafc',
      padding: '16px'
    }}>
      <div style={{
        background: '#1e293b',
        border: '1px solid #334155',
        borderRadius: '16px',
        width: '100%',
        maxWidth: '400px',
        padding: '32px',
        boxShadow: '0 20px 25px -5px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)'
      }}>
        
        {/* LOGO Y TÃTULO */}
        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: '28px' }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: '36px', marginBottom: '8px' }}>âš¡</div>
          <h1 style={{ fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: '800', margin: 0 }}>Rendix Cloud</h1>
          <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px', marginTop: '6px' }}>
            {isSignUp ? 'Crea tu cuenta para gestionar tu garaje' : 'Ingresa a tu cuenta'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* MENSAJES DE ERROR / Ã‰XITO */}
        {message.text && (
          <div style={{
            padding: '10px 14px',
            borderRadius: '8px',
            marginBottom: '16px',
            fontSize: '13px',
            backgroundColor: message.type === 'error' ? 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)' : 'rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.15)',
            color: message.type === 'error' ? '#fca5a5' : '#86efac',
            border: `1px solid ${message.type === 'error' ? 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3)' : 'rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.3)'}`
          }}>
            {message.text}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* FORMULARIO */}
        <form onSubmit={handleAuth} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
          <div>
            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#cbd5e1', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
              Correo electrÃ³nico
            </label>
            <input
              required
              type="email"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="tu@correo.com"
              style={inputStyle}
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#cbd5e1', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
              ContraseÃ±a
            </label>
            <input
              required
              type="password"
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢"
              style={inputStyle}
            />
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading}
            style={{
              width: '100%',
              padding: '12px',
              backgroundColor: '#4f46e5',
              color: '#ffffff',
              border: 'none',
              borderRadius: '8px',
              fontWeight: '700',
              cursor: loading ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
              marginTop: '8px',
              boxShadow: '0 4px 6px -1px rgba(79, 70, 229, 0.3)',
              transition: 'background 0.2s'
            }}
          >
            {loading ? 'Cargando...' : isSignUp ? 'Registrarse' : 'Iniciar SesiÃ³n'}
          </button>
        </form>

        {/* CAMBIO ENTRE LOGIN Y REGISTRO */}
        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: '20px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
          {isSignUp ? 'Â¿Ya tienes una cuenta?' : 'Â¿No tienes cuenta todavÃ­a?'}{' '}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setIsSignUp(!isSignUp);
              setMessage({ text: '', type: '' });
            }}
            style={{
              background: 'none',
              border: 'none',
              color: '#818cf8',
              fontWeight: '700',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              padding: 0
            }}
          >
            {isSignUp ? 'Inicia sesiÃ³n' : 'RegÃ­strate aquÃ­'}
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

const inputStyle = {
  width: '100%',
  padding: '10px 14px',
  borderRadius: '8px',
  border: '1px solid #334155',
  backgroundColor: '#0f172a',
  color: '#f8fafc',
  fontSize: '14px',
  outline: 'none',
  boxSizing: 'border-box'
};


==================== ARCHIVO: src\components\EfficiencyInputCard.jsx ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: src/components/EfficiencyInputCard.jsx
  PropÃ³sito: Tarjeta interactiva para ingresar el rendimiento desde el computador
  a bordo del vehÃ­culo con conversor automÃ¡tico km/L <-> L/100km y guÃ­a rÃ¡pida.
*/

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export default function EfficiencyInputCard({
  value = '',
  onChange,
  onSave = null,
  vehicleName = '',
  saving = false
}) {
  const [unit, setUnit] = useState('kml'); // 'kml' | 'l100'
  const [rawInput, setRawInput] = useState(value ? String(value) : '');
  const [showTip, setShowTip] = useState(false);

 useEffect(() => {
    if (!value) {
      setRawInput('');
      return;
    }
    const num = Number(value);
    if (num > 0) {
      const currentRaw = Number(rawInput);
      if (unit === 'kml' && currentRaw === num) return;
      if (unit === 'l100' && currentRaw > 0 && Math.abs(Number((100 / currentRaw).toFixed(2)) - num) < 0.02) {
        return;
      }
      setRawInput(unit === 'kml' ? String(num) : (100 / num).toFixed(1));
    }
  }, [value]);

  const handleUnitSwitch = (newUnit) => {
    if (newUnit === unit) return;
    const currentNum = Number(rawInput);
    if (currentNum > 0) {
      setRawInput((100 / currentNum).toFixed(1));
    }
    setUnit(newUnit);
  };

  const handleInputChange = (e) => {
    const valStr = e.target.value;
    setRawInput(valStr);

    const num = Number(valStr);
    if (!valStr || num <= 0) {
      if (onChange) onChange('');
      return;
    }

    const normalizedKmL = unit === 'kml' ? num : Number((100 / num).toFixed(2));
    if (onChange) onChange(normalizedKmL);
  };

  const numericRaw = Number(rawInput);
  const convertedPreview =
    numericRaw > 0
      ? unit === 'kml'
        ? `${(100 / numericRaw).toFixed(1)} L/100km`
        : `${(100 / numericRaw).toFixed(1)} km/L`
      : null;

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        background: '#0f172a',
        border: '1px solid #334155',
        borderRadius: '12px',
        padding: '14px',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        gap: '10px'
      }}
    >
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <div>
          <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '800', color: '#38bdf8', display: 'block' }}>
            ðŸ“Ÿ RENDIMIENTO COMPUTADOR A BORDO {vehicleName ? `(${vehicleName})` : ''}
          </span>
          <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
            Respaldo inteligente cuando aÃºn no tienes 2 estanques llenos.
          </span>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', background: '#1e293b', padding: '3px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #334155' }}>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleUnitSwitch('kml')}
            style={{
              padding: '4px 10px',
              borderRadius: '6px',
              border: 'none',
              fontSize: '11px',
              fontWeight: '800',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              background: unit === 'kml' ? '#3b82f6' : 'transparent',
              color: unit === 'kml' ? '#ffffff' : '#94a3b8'
            }}
          >
            km/L
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleUnitSwitch('l100')}
            style={{
              padding: '4px 10px',
              borderRadius: '6px',
              border: 'none',
              fontSize: '11px',
              fontWeight: '800',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              background: unit === 'l100' ? '#3b82f6' : 'transparent',
              color: unit === 'l100' ? '#ffffff' : '#94a3b8'
            }}
          >
            L/100km
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <div style={{ position: 'relative', flex: 1 }}>
          <input
            type="number"
            step="0.1"
            min="0"
            placeholder={unit === 'kml' ? 'Ej: 13.5' : 'Ej: 7.4'}
            value={rawInput}
            onChange={handleInputChange}
            style={{
              width: '100%',
              background: '#1e293b',
              border: '1px solid #334155',
              borderRadius: '8px',
              padding: '10px 70px 10px 12px',
              color: '#f8fafc',
              fontSize: '14px',
              fontWeight: '800',
              outline: 'none',
              boxSizing: 'border-box'
            }}
          />
          <span
            style={{
              position: 'absolute',
              right: '12px',
              top: '50%',
              transform: 'translateY(-50%)',
              fontSize: '11px',
              fontWeight: '800',
              color: '#38bdf8'
            }}
          >
            {unit === 'kml' ? 'km/L' : 'L/100km'}
          </span>
        </div>

        {onSave && (
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={saving || !numericRaw}
            onClick={() => onSave(unit === 'kml' ? numericRaw : Number((100 / numericRaw).toFixed(2)))}
            style={{
              background: numericRaw > 0 ? '#10b981' : '#334155',
              color: '#ffffff',
              border: 'none',
              padding: '10px 14px',
              borderRadius: '8px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontWeight: '800',
              cursor: numericRaw > 0 ? 'pointer' : 'not-allowed',
              whiteSpace: 'nowrap'
            }}
          >
            {saving ? 'Guardando...' : 'ðŸ’¾ Guardar'}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', fontSize: '11px' }}>
        {convertedPreview ? (
          <span style={{ color: '#4ade80', fontWeight: '700' }}>
            ðŸ”„ Equivalencia: <strong>{convertedPreview}</strong>
          </span>
        ) : (
          <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8' }}>Opcional â€¢ Se guarda normalizado en km/L</span>
        )}

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setShowTip(!showTip)}
          style={{
            background: 'none',
            border: 'none',
            color: '#38bdf8',
            fontSize: '11px',
            fontWeight: '700',
            cursor: 'pointer',
            padding: 0
          }}
        >
          {showTip ? 'Ocultar ayuda â–²' : 'ðŸ’¡ Â¿DÃ³nde verlo en mi auto?'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {showTip && (
        <div
          style={{
            background: 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.08)',
            border: '1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.25)',
            borderRadius: '8px',
            padding: '8px 10px',
            fontSize: '11px',
            color: '#cbd5e1',
            lineHeight: '1.4'
          }}
        >
          Presiona el botÃ³n <strong>TRIP / DISP / INFO</strong> en el volante o tablero hasta ver el indicador <strong>AVG</strong>. Si tu auto marca en <strong>L/100km</strong> (ej. Haval, Peugeot, Chery), selecciona esa unidad arriba y Rendix lo convertirÃ¡ a <strong>km/L</strong> automÃ¡ticamente.
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';

export default function Toast({ toasts = [] }) {
  if (!toasts.length) return null;

  return (

      position: 'fixed',

      right: '24px',
      display: 'flex',
      flexDirection: 'column',

      zIndex: 9999,
      pointerEvents: 'none'
    }}>
      {toasts.map(toast => (
        <div key={toast.id} style={{
          background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #1e293b, #0f172a)',
          color: '#f8fafc',
          padding: '14px 20px',
          borderRadius: '12px',

          boxShadow: '0 10px 25px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)',

          fontWeight: '700',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: '10px',
          pointerEvents: 'auto',
          animation: 'slideInUp 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1)'
        }}>
          <span style={{ color: '#38bdf8', fontSize: '16px' }}>âœ¨</span>
          {toast.message}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}


==================== ARCHIVO: src\modals\AnalyticsModal.jsx ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: AnalyticsModal.jsx
  PropÃ³sito: Modal analÃ­tico con grÃ¡ficos de lÃ­neas (Recharts) para evaluar 
  la tendencia mensual de combustible y costos operativos.
*/

import React from 'react';
import { ResponsiveContainer, LineChart, Line, XAxis, YAxis, Tooltip, CartesianGrid, Legend } from 'recharts';

export default function AnalyticsModal({ fuelRecords = [], expenses = [], onClose }) {

  // Procesar y agrupar los datos por mes para alimentar el grÃ¡fico
  const dataMap = {};

  fuelRecords.forEach(f => {
    const month = f.date ? f.date.substring(0, 7) : 'Desconocido'; // Formato YYYY-MM
    if (!dataMap[month]) dataMap[month] = { month, bencina: 0, gastos: 0 };
    dataMap[month].bencina += Number(f.total || 0);
  });

  expenses.forEach(e => {
    const month = e.date ? e.date.substring(0, 7) : 'Desconocido';
    if (!dataMap[month]) dataMap[month] = { month, bencina: 0, gastos: 0 };
    dataMap[month].gastos += Number(e.amount || 0);
  });

  // Convertir a array y ordenar cronolÃ³gicamente por mes
  const chartData = Object.values(dataMap).sort((a, b) => a.month.localeCompare(b.month));

  return (
    <div style={{
      position: 'fixed', top: 0, left: 0, width: '100%', height: '100%',
      backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.75)', display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center',
      alignItems: 'center', zIndex: 1000, backdropFilter: 'blur(5px)'
    }}>
      <div style={{
        background: '#1e293b', border: '1px solid #334155', borderRadius: '16px',
        padding: '30px', width: '100%', maxWidth: '850px', boxShadow: '0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)',
        display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px', boxSizing: 'border-box'
      }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <div>
            <h3 style={{ margin: 0, color: '#f8fafc', fontSize: '22px', fontWeight: '800' }}>
              ðŸ“ˆ AnalÃ­tica y Tendencia de Gastos
            </h3>
            <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 0', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px' }}>
              EvoluciÃ³n mensual consolidada de combustible y costos operativos de la flota.
            </p>
          </div>
          <button 
            onClick={onClose} 
            style={{ background: 'transparent', border: 'none', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '20px', cursor: 'pointer' }}
          >
            âœ•
          </button>
        </div>

        {chartData.length === 0 ? (
          <div style={{ padding: '40px', textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8', background: '#0f172a', borderRadius: '12px' }}>
            No hay suficientes registros histÃ³ricos para generar las curvas analÃ­ticas.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div style={{ background: '#0f172a', padding: '20px', borderRadius: '12px', border: '1px solid #334155', height: '340px' }}>
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <LineChart data={chartData}>
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#334155" />
                <XAxis dataKey="month" stroke="#94a3b8" fontSize={12} />
                <YAxis stroke="#94a3b8" fontSize={12} tickFormatter={(val) => `$${val.toLocaleString('es-CL')}`} />
                <Tooltip 
                  contentStyle={{ background: '#1e293b', borderColor: '#334155', borderRadius: '8px', color: '#f8fafc' }}
                  formatter={(val) => [`$${Number(val).toLocaleString('es-CL')}`, '']}
                />
                <Legend wrapperStyle={{ paddingTop: '10px' }} />
                <Line type="monotone" dataKey="bencina" name="Gasto Bencina" stroke="#38bdf8" strokeWidth={3} dot={{ fill: '#38bdf8' }} />
                <Line type="monotone" dataKey="gastos" name="Gastos Operativos" stroke="#fca5a5" strokeWidth={3} dot={{ fill: '#fca5a5' }} />
              </LineChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        )}

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
          <button 
            onClick={onClose} 
            style={{ background: '#334155', color: '#f8fafc', border: 'none', padding: '10px 20px', borderRadius: '8px', fontWeight: '700', cursor: 'pointer' }}
          >
            Cerrar AnalÃ­tica
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


==================== ARCHIVO: src\modals\ExpenseModal.jsx ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: ExpenseModal.jsx
  PropÃ³sito: Componente modal que provee el formulario para registrar un nuevo gasto operativo 
  (TAG, mantenciÃ³n, seguro, etc.) asociado a un vehÃ­culo en Supabase.
*/

import React, { useState } from 'react';

export default function ExpenseModal({ vehicles = [], onClose, onSubmit }) {
  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    vehicleId: vehicles[0]?.id || '',
    date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
    category: 'TAG / Peajes',
    description: '',
    amount: ''
  });

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const categories = [
    'TAG / Peajes',
    'Mantenimiento',
    'Seguros / Permisos',
    'Estacionamiento',
    'Lavado / EstÃ©tica',
    'Repuestos / Accesorios',
    'Otro'
  ];

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!form.vehicleId) {
      alert('Debes seleccionar o tener registrado al menos un vehÃ­culo.');
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    await onSubmit(form);
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div style={{
      position: 'fixed',
      top: 0,
      left: 0,
      right: 0,
      bottom: 0,
      backgroundColor: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.75)',
      backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)',
      display: 'flex',
      alignItems: 'center',
      justifyContent: 'center',
      zIndex: 1000,
      padding: '16px'
    }}>
      <div style={{
        background: '#1e293b',
        border: '1px solid #334155',
        borderRadius: '16px',
        width: '100%',
        maxWidth: '440px',
        boxShadow: '0 20px 25px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)',
        color: '#f8fafc',
        overflow: 'hidden'
      }}>
        
        {/* Cabecera del Modal */}
        <div style={{
          padding: '20px 24px',
          borderBottom: '1px solid #334155',
          display: 'flex',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          alignItems: 'center'
        }}>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: '700', margin: 0, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
            <span>ðŸ’¸</span> Registrar Gasto Operativo
          </h2>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            style={{
              background: 'transparent',
              border: 'none',
              color: '#94a3b8',
              fontSize: '20px',
              cursor: 'pointer'
            }}
          >
            âœ•
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Formulario */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} style={{ padding: '24px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
          
          {/* VehÃ­culo */}
          <div>
            <label style={labelStyle}>VehÃ­culo *</label>
            {vehicles.length === 0 ? (
              <p style={{ color: '#fca5a5', fontSize: '13px', margin: '4px 0 0 0' }}>
                âš ï¸ Primero debes agregar un vehÃ­culo en "Mis VehÃ­culos".
              </p>
            ) : (
              <select
                required
                value={form.vehicleId}
                onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, vehicleId: e.target.value })}
                style={{ ...inputStyle, cursor: 'pointer' }}
              >
                {vehicles.map(v => (
                  <option key={v.id} value={v.id} style={{ background: '#1e293b' }}>
                    {v.name} ({v.brand} {v.model})
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* CategorÃ­a y Fecha */}
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '12px' }}>
            <div>
              <label style={labelStyle}>CategorÃ­a *</label>
              <select
                required
                value={form.category}
                onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, category: e.target.value })}
                style={{ ...inputStyle, cursor: 'pointer' }}
              >
                {categories.map((cat, idx) => (
                  <option key={idx} value={cat} style={{ background: '#1e293b' }}>
                    {cat}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label style={labelStyle}>Fecha *</label>
              <input
                required
                type="date"
                value={form.date}
                onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, date: e.target.value })}
                style={inputStyle}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* DescripciÃ³n */}
          <div>
            <label style={labelStyle}>DescripciÃ³n / Detalle</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={form.description}
              onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, description: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="Ej: Peajes Costanera Norte, Cambio aceite"
              style={inputStyle}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Monto ($) */}
          <div>
            <label style={labelStyle}>Monto Total ($) *</label>
            <input
              required
              type="number"
              min="1"
              value={form.amount}
              onChange={e => setForm({ ...form, amount: e.target.value })}
              placeholder="Ej: 15000"
              style={inputStyle}
            />
          </div>

          {/* Botones de AcciÃ³n */}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '12px', marginTop: '8px' }}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                padding: '12px',
                background: '#334155',
                color: '#f8fafc',
                border: 'none',
                borderRadius: '8px',
                fontWeight: '600',
                cursor: 'pointer'
              }}
            >
              Cancelar
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading || vehicles.length === 0}
              style={{
                flex: 1,
                padding: '12px',
                background: vehicles.length === 0 ? '#64748b' : '#4f46e5',
                color: '#ffffff',
                border: 'none',
                borderRadius: '8px',
                fontWeight: '600',
                cursor: vehicles.length === 0 ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
                boxShadow: '0 4px 6px -1px rgba(79, 70, 229, 0.3)'
              }}
            >
              {loading ? 'Guardando...' : 'Guardar Gasto'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

const labelStyle = { display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#cbd5e1', marginBottom: '6px' };
const inputStyle = { width: '100%', padding: '10px 14px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #334155', background: '#0f172a', color: '#f8fafc', fontSize: '14px', outline: 'none', boxSizing: 'border-box' };

==================== ARCHIVO: src\modals\TripCalculatorModal.jsx ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: TripCalculatorModal.jsx
  PropÃ³sito: Calculadora de viajes y cotizaciÃ³n de fletes para RendixApp 
  con rendimiento dinÃ¡mico calculado por vehÃ­culo y mapa interactivo Leaflet.
*/

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { MapContainer, TileLayer, Marker, Popup, useMapEvents } from 'react-leaflet';
import L from 'leaflet';

delete L.Icon.Default.prototype._getIconUrl;
L.Icon.Default.mergeOptions({
  iconRetinaUrl: 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/leaflet/1.7.1/images/marker-icon-2x.png',
  iconUrl: 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/leaflet/1.7.1/images/marker-icon.png',
  shadowUrl: 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/leaflet/1.7.1/images/marker-shadow.png',
});

function MapClickHandler({ origin, setOrigin, setDestination, setDistanceKm }) {
  useMapEvents({
    click(e) {
      const { lat, lng } = e.latlng;
      if (!origin) {
        setOrigin([lat, lng]);
        setDestination(null);
        setDistanceKm('0.0');
      } else {
        setDestination([lat, lng]);
        const originLatLng = L.latLng(origin);
        const destLatLng = L.latLng(lat, lng);
        const distanceMeters = originLatLng.distanceTo(destLatLng);
        const approxKm = ((distanceMeters / 1000) * 1.25).toFixed(1);
        setDistanceKm(approxKm);
      }
    }
  });
  return null;
}

export default function TripCalculatorModal({ vehicles = [], fuelRecords = [], onClose }) {
  const [selectedVehicleId, setSelectedVehicleId] = useState(vehicles[0]?.id || '');
  const [distanceKm, setDistanceKm] = useState('25.0');
  const [fuelPrice, setFuelPrice] = useState('1320');
  const [tolls, setTolls] = useState('3500');
  const [wearPerKm, setWearPerKm] = useState('45');
  const [marginPercent, setMarginPercent] = useState('35');

  const [origin, setOrigin] = useState([-33.411, -70.578]);
  const [destination, setDestination] = useState([-33.448, -70.650]);

  // FUNCIÃ“N PARA CALCULAR EL RENDIMIENTO REAL DE CADA VEHÃCULO (km/L)
  const calculateVehicleKmLiter = (vId) => {
    if (!fuelRecords || fuelRecords.length === 0 || !vId) return 10.0; // Valor fallback por defecto si no hay cargas

    const vRecords = fuelRecords.filter(r => (r.vehicle_id || r.vehicleId) === vId);
    if (vRecords.length < 2) {
      // Si sÃ³lo tiene 1 carga, intenta estimar con el odÃ³metro actual del vehÃ­culo o retorna fallback
      return 10.0;
    }

    const sorted = [...vRecords].sort((a, b) => Number(a.odometer) - Number(b.odometer));
    let totalKmTraveled = 0;
    let totalLitersConsumed = 0;

    for (let i = 1; i < sorted.length; i++) {
      const kmDiff = Number(sorted[i].odometer) - Number(sorted[i - 1].odometer);
      const liters = Number(sorted[i].liters);
      if (kmDiff > 0 && liters > 0) {
        totalKmTraveled += kmDiff;
        totalLitersConsumed += liters;
      }
    }

    if (totalLitersConsumed === 0) return 10.0;
    return Number((totalKmTraveled / totalLitersConsumed).toFixed(1));
  };

  // Rendimiento dinÃ¡mico del vehÃ­culo seleccionado
  const vehicleKmLiter = calculateVehicleKmLiter(selectedVehicleId);

  // CÃLCULOS FINANCIEROS
  const distNum = Number(distanceKm) || 0;
  const priceNum = Number(fuelPrice) || 0;
  const tollsNum = Number(tolls) || 0;
  const wearNum = Number(wearPerKm) || 0;
  const marginNum = Number(marginPercent) || 0;

  const litersNeeded = vehicleKmLiter > 0 ? distNum / vehicleKmLiter : 0;
  const fuelCostTotal = litersNeeded * priceNum;
  const wearCostTotal = distNum * wearNum;
  const directOperatingCost = fuelCostTotal + tollsNum + wearCostTotal;
  const suggestedFreightTariff = directOperatingCost * (1 + marginNum / 100);
  const netProfit = suggestedFreightTariff - directOperatingCost;

  return (
    <div style={{
      position: 'fixed', top: 0, left: 0, width: '100%', height: '100%',
      backgroundColor: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.8)', display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center',
      alignItems: 'center', zIndex: 9999, backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)', padding: '16px'
    }}>
      <div style={{
        background: '#1e293b', border: '1px solid #334155', borderRadius: '16px',
        width: '100%', maxWidth: '980px', maxHeight: '92vh', overflowY: 'auto',
        color: '#f8fafc', boxShadow: '0 25px 50px -12px rgba(0,0,0,0.6)', padding: '24px'
      }}>
        
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '16px', borderBottom: '1px solid #334155', paddingBottom: '12px' }}>
          <div>
            <h2 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '22px', fontWeight: '800', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
              <span>ðŸ—ºï¸</span> Calculadora de Viajes y CotizaciÃ³n de Fletes
            </h2>
            <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 0', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px' }}>
              RendixApp â€¢ Desglose transparente de combustible, desgaste y margen comercial.
            </p>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} style={{ background: 'transparent', border: 'none', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '20px', cursor: 'pointer' }}>âœ•</button>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1.2fr 1fr', gap: '20px' }}>
          
          {/* MAPA */}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '10px' }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '700', color: '#cbd5e1' }}>
                ðŸ“ Marca Origen (1er Clic) y Destino (2do Clic)
              </span>
              <button 
                onClick={() => { setOrigin(null); setDestination(null); setDistanceKm('0'); }}
                style={{ background: '#334155', color: '#94a3b8', border: 'none', padding: '4px 8px', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '11px', cursor: 'pointer' }}
              >
                Reiniciar Marcas
              </button>
            </div>

            <div style={{ height: '360px', borderRadius: '12px', overflow: 'hidden', border: '1px solid #334155' }}>
              <MapContainer center={[-33.4489, -70.6693]} zoom={11} style={{ height: '100%', width: '100%' }}>
                <TileLayer
                  attribution='&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a>'
                  url="https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png"
                />
                <MapClickHandler 
                  origin={origin} 
                  setOrigin={setOrigin} 
                  setDestination={setDestination} 
                  setDistanceKm={setDistanceKm} 
                />

                {origin && (
                  <Marker position={origin}>
                    <Popup>ðŸŸ¢ Origen del Viaje</Popup>
                  </Marker>
                )}
                {destination && (
                  <Marker position={destination}>
                    <Popup>ðŸ”´ Destino del Viaje</Popup>
                  </Marker>
                )}
              </MapContainer>
            </div>

            <div style={{ background: '#0f172a', padding: '12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #334155', display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <label style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8', fontWeight: '700' }}>Distancia Calculada / Manual (KM):</label>
              <input
                type="number"
                step="0.1"
                value={distanceKm}
                onChange={(e) => setDistanceKm(e.target.value)}
                style={{ width: '90px', padding: '6px', background: '#1e293b', border: '1px solid #38bdf8', color: '#38bdf8', borderRadius: '6px', fontWeight: '800', textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '15px' }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* PARÃMETROS */}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px' }}>
            
            <div>
              <label style={labelStyle}>VehÃ­culo de la Flota *</label>
              <select
                value={selectedVehicleId}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedVehicleId(e.target.value)}
                style={inputStyle}
              >
                {vehicles.map(v => {
                  const rend = calculateVehicleKmLiter(v.id);
                  return (
                    <option key={v.id} value={v.id} style={{ background: '#1e293b' }}>
                      {v.name} ({v.plate || 'Sin Patente'}) - Rend: {rend} km/L
                    </option>
                  );
                })}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '10px' }}>
              <div>
                <label style={labelStyle}>Precio Bencina ($/L)</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={fuelPrice}
                  onChange={(e) => setFuelPrice(e.target.value)}
                  style={inputStyle}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label style={labelStyle}>Peajes / TAG ($)</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={tolls}
                  onChange={(e) => setTolls(e.target.value)}
                  style={inputStyle}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '10px' }}>
              <div>
                <label style={labelStyle}>Reserva Desgaste ($/km)</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={wearPerKm}
                  onChange={(e) => setWearPerKm(e.target.value)}
                  style={inputStyle}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label style={labelStyle}>Margen Ganancia (%)</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={marginPercent}
                  onChange={(e) => setMarginPercent(e.target.value)}
                  style={inputStyle}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div style={{ background: '#0f172a', padding: '14px', borderRadius: '10px', border: '1px solid #334155', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px', marginTop: '4px' }}>
              
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8' }}>Combustible Requerido:</span>
                <strong style={{ color: '#f8fafc' }}>{litersNeeded.toFixed(2)} Litros</strong>
              </div>

              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8' }}>Gasto Bencina + Peajes:</span>
                <strong style={{ color: '#fca5a5' }}>${Math.round(fuelCostTotal + tollsNum).toLocaleString('es-CL')}</strong>
              </div>

              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8' }}>Reserva MantenciÃ³n / NeumÃ¡ticos:</span>
                <strong style={{ color: '#eab308' }}>${Math.round(wearCostTotal).toLocaleString('es-CL')}</strong>
              </div>

              <hr style={{ borderColor: '#334155', margin: '4px 0' }} />

              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: '13px' }}>
                <span style={{ color: '#cbd5e1', fontWeight: '700' }}>COSTO OPERATIVO REAL:</span>
                <strong style={{ color: '#38bdf8', fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: '800' }}>
                  ${Math.round(directOperatingCost).toLocaleString('es-CL')}
                </strong>
              </div>

              <div style={{ background: 'rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.15)', border: '1px solid rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.4)', padding: '10px', borderRadius: '8px', display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginTop: '4px' }}>
                <div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: '10px', color: '#4ade80', fontWeight: '800', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>TARIFA SUGERIDA DE FLETE</div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#cbd5e1' }}>Ganancia Neta: +${Math.round(netProfit).toLocaleString('es-CL')}</div>
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '20px', fontWeight: '900', color: '#22c55e' }}>
                  ${Math.round(suggestedFreightTariff).toLocaleString('es-CL')}
                </div>
              </div>

            </div>

          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', marginTop: '16px' }}>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            style={{ background: '#334155', color: '#f8fafc', border: 'none', padding: '10px 24px', borderRadius: '8px', fontWeight: '700', cursor: 'pointer' }}
          >
            Cerrar Calculadora
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

const inputStyle = { width: '100%', padding: '8px 10px', borderRadius: '6px', border: '1px solid #334155', background: '#0f172a', color: '#f8fafc', fontSize: '13px', outline: 'none', boxSizing: 'border-box' };

==================== ARCHIVO: src\services\cneService.js ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: cneService.js
  PropÃ³sito: AutenticaciÃ³n contra api.cne.cl vÃ­a proxy (token vÃ¡lido por 1 hora con cachÃ©
  preventivo de 55 min), timeout de 6s, cooldown ante errores 504 y normalizaciÃ³n de precios.
*/

const BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_CNE_BASE_URL || '/cne-api';
const CNE_EMAIL = import.meta.env.VITE_CNE_EMAIL || 'ma.canelo@duocuc.cl';
const CNE_PASSWORD = import.meta.env.VITE_CNE_PASSWORD || 'd000f39e8c';

const TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY = 'rendix_cne_token';
const TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY = 'rendix_cne_token_expiry';
const COOLDOWN_KEY = 'rendix_cne_cooldown_until';

// 55 minutos de vigencia en cachÃ© (margen de 5 min respecto a la hora real)
const TOKEN_TTL_MS = 55 * 60 * 1000;
// 10 minutos de pausa si la API externa devuelve 504 o no responde
const ERROR_COOLDOWN_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;
// MÃ¡ximo 6 segundos de espera por peticiÃ³n
const REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 6000;

async function fetchWithTimeout(url, options = {}, timeoutMs = REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timerId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);

  try {
    return await fetch(url, {
      ...options,
      signal: controller.signal
    });
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timerId);
  }
}

/**
 * Solicita un nuevo token a POST /api/login y lo almacena por 55 minutos.
 */
async function loginCneApi() {
  const response = await fetchWithTimeout(`${BASE_URL}/api/login`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'Accept': 'application/json'
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      email: CNE_EMAIL,
      password: CNE_PASSWORD
    })
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status} en /api/login`);
  }

  const data = await response.json();
  const token =
    typeof data === 'string'
      ? data
      : data?.token || data?.access_token || data?.data?.token;

  if (!token) {
    throw new Error('Respuesta de /api/login sin token vÃ¡lido');
  }

  localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY, token);
  localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY, String(Date.now() + TOKEN_TTL_MS));
  localStorage.removeItem(COOLDOWN_KEY);

  return token;
}

/**
 * Devuelve un token vigente desde cachÃ© o solicita uno nuevo si expirÃ³.
 */
export async function getValidCneToken(forceRefresh = false) {
  if (!forceRefresh) {
    const cachedToken = localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY);
    const expiresAt = Number(localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY) || 0);

    if (cachedToken && Date.now() < expiresAt) {
      return cachedToken;
    }
  }

  return await loginCneApi();
}

/**
 * Normaliza la estructura de cada estaciÃ³n al formato usado por CneView y FuelView.
 */
function normalizeCneStation(raw, index) {
  const ubicacion = raw.ubicacion || {};
  const distribuidor = raw.distribuidor || {};
  const precios = raw.precios || {};

  const extractPrice = (keys) => {
    for (const k of keys) {
      const val = precios[k] ?? raw[k];
      if (val !== undefined && val !== null) {
        const numeric = typeof val === 'object' ? Number(val.precio || val.valor || 0) : Number(val);
        if (numeric > 0) return numeric;
      }
    }
    return 0;
  };

  const brand = distribuidor.marca || distribuidor.nombre || raw.marca || 'EstaciÃ³n CNE';
  const address = ubicacion.direccion || raw.direccion || 'DirecciÃ³n no informada';

  return {
    id: raw.codigo || raw.id || `cne-${index}`,
    name: raw.razon_social || raw.nombre || `${brand} - ${address}`,
    brand,
    region: ubicacion.nombre_region || raw.region || '',
    commune: ubicacion.nombre_comuna || raw.comuna || 'Otra Comuna',
    address,
    p93: extractPrice(['93', 'gasolina_93', 'p93']),
    p95: extractPrice(['95', 'gasolina_95', 'p95']),
    p97: extractPrice(['97', 'gasolina_97', 'p97']),
    diesel: extractPrice(['DI', 'diesel', 'petroleo_diesel']),
    kerosene: extractPrice(['KE', 'kerosene', 'parafina'])
  };
}

/**
 * Consulta precios en vivo desde la API CNE usando el token cacheado.
 */
export async function fetchRealCnePrices({ regionFilter = 'Metropolitana', forceRetry = false } = {}) {
  if (!forceRetry) {
    const cooldownUntil = Number(localStorage.getItem(COOLDOWN_KEY) || 0);
    if (Date.now() < cooldownUntil) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  const executeRequest = async (token) => {
    return await fetchWithTimeout(`${BASE_URL}/api/v4/estaciones`, {
      method: 'GET',
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
        'Accept': 'application/json'
      }
    });
  };

  try {
    let token = await getValidCneToken(false);
    let response = await executeRequest(token);

    if (response.status === 401) {
      token = await getValidCneToken(true);
      response = await executeRequest(token);
    }

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status} al consultar estaciones`);
    }

    const payload = await response.json();
    const rawList = Array.isArray(payload) ? payload : payload?.data || payload?.estaciones || [];
    const normalized = rawList.map(normalizeCneStation);

    if (regionFilter) {
      const filteredByRegion = normalized.filter((st) =>
        st.region.toLowerCase().includes(regionFilter.toLowerCase())
      );
      return filteredByRegion.length > 0 ? filteredByRegion : normalized;
    }

    return normalized;
  } catch (error) {
    // Activar pausa de 10 minutos para no bloquear la UI si api.cne.cl estÃ¡ caÃ­da o en 504
    localStorage.setItem(COOLDOWN_KEY, String(Date.now() + ERROR_COOLDOWN_MS));
    console.warn(`[CNE] API externa no disponible (${error.message}). Usando respaldo local.`);
    return null;
  }
}


==================== ARCHIVO: src\services\googleMapsService.js ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: src/services/googleMapsService.js
  PropÃ³sito: Carga Google Maps JS API y conecta Routes API v2 (trazado real por calles)
  junto con Places API (New) para bÃºsqueda de direcciones en Chile.
*/

const API_KEY = import.meta.env.VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY;
let mapsLoaderPromise = null;

export function hasGoogleMapsKey() {
  return Boolean(API_KEY && API_KEY.trim().length > 10);
}

export function loadGoogleMaps() {
  if (!hasGoogleMapsKey()) {
    return Promise.reject(new Error('Falta VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY en el archivo .env'));
  }

  if (window.google?.maps?.geometry) {
    return Promise.resolve(window.google.maps);
  }

  if (mapsLoaderPromise) {
    return mapsLoaderPromise;
  }

  mapsLoaderPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const existingScript = document.querySelector('script[data-google-maps="true"]');
    if (existingScript) {
      existingScript.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.google.maps));
      existingScript.addEventListener('error', () => reject(new Error('Error al cargar Google Maps')));
      return;
    }

    const script = document.createElement('script');
    script.src = `https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/js?key=${API_KEY}&libraries=geometry&language=es&region=CL`;
    script.async = true;
    script.defer = true;
    script.dataset.googleMaps = 'true';

    script.onload = () => {
      if (window.google?.maps) {
        resolve(window.google.maps);
      } else {
        reject(new Error('Google Maps no se inicializÃ³ correctamente'));
      }
    };

    script.onerror = () => {
      mapsLoaderPromise = null;
      reject(new Error('No se pudo cargar Google Maps. Revisa tu API Key y restricciones.'));
    };

    document.head.appendChild(script);
  });

  return mapsLoaderPromise;
}

export async function computeStreetRoute(origin, destination) {
  if (!hasGoogleMapsKey()) {
    throw new Error('Falta VITE_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY en .env');
  }

  const response = await fetch('https://routes.googleapis.com/directions/v2:computeRoutes', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'X-Goog-Api-Key': API_KEY,
      'X-Goog-FieldMask': 'routes.duration,routes.distanceMeters,routes.polyline.encodedPolyline'
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      origin: {
        location: {
          latLng: { latitude: Number(origin.lat), longitude: Number(origin.lng) }
        }
      },
      destination: {
        location: {
          latLng: { latitude: Number(destination.lat), longitude: Number(destination.lng) }
        }
      },
      travelMode: 'DRIVE',
      routingPreference: 'TRAFFIC_AWARE',
      languageCode: 'es-CL',
      units: 'METRIC'
    })
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    const errData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(errData?.error?.message || `Error Routes API (${response.status})`);
  }

  const data = await response.json();
  const route = data?.routes?.[0];
  if (!route) {
    throw new Error('No se encontrÃ³ una ruta vehicular entre esos puntos.');
  }

  const distanceMeters = Number(route.distanceMeters || 0);
  const distanceKm = Number((distanceMeters / 1000).toFixed(1));
  const durationSeconds = parseInt(String(route.duration || '0s').replace('s', ''), 10) || 0;

  return {
    distanceKm,
    durationSeconds,
    durationText: formatDuration(durationSeconds),
    encodedPolyline: route.polyline?.encodedPolyline || ''
  };
}

export async function searchPlacesChile(query) {
  if (!hasGoogleMapsKey() || !query || query.trim().length < 3) {
    return [];
  }

  const response = await fetch('https://places.googleapis.com/v1/places:searchText', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'X-Goog-Api-Key': API_KEY,
      'X-Goog-FieldMask': 'places.id,places.displayName,places.formattedAddress,places.location'
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      textQuery: query.trim(),
      languageCode: 'es',
      regionCode: 'CL',
      maxResultCount: 5
    })
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    return [];
  }

  const data = await response.json();
  return (data.places || [])
    .filter(p => p.location)
    .map(p => ({
      id: p.id,
      name: p.displayName?.text || '',
      address: p.formattedAddress || '',
      lat: p.location.latitude,
      lng: p.location.longitude
    }));
}

function formatDuration(totalSeconds) {
  if (!totalSeconds || totalSeconds <= 0) return '';
  const mins = Math.round(totalSeconds / 60);
  if (mins < 60) return `${mins} min`;
  const hrs = Math.floor(mins / 60);
  const remMins = mins % 60;
  return remMins > 0 ? `${hrs} h ${remMins} min` : `${hrs} h`;
}


==================== ARCHIVO: src\utils\efficiencyHelper.js ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: src/utils/efficiencyHelper.js
  PropÃ³sito: Obtiene el rendimiento manual (km/L) configurado en cada vehÃ­culo
  y calcula el promedio general de la flota.
*/

export function getManualEfficiency(vehicle) {
  if (!vehicle) return 0;
  const val = Number(vehicle.manual_efficiency || 0);
  return val > 0 ? Number(val.toFixed(1)) : 0;
}

export function calculateFleetAverageEfficiency(vehicles = []) {
  if (!Array.isArray(vehicles) || vehicles.length === 0) return '0.0';

  const validValues = vehicles
    .map(v => getManualEfficiency(v))
    .filter(val => val > 0);

  if (validValues.length === 0) return '0.0';

  const sum = validValues.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + curr, 0);
  return (sum / validValues.length).toFixed(1);
}

export function calculateHybridEfficiency(vehicle) {
  const kmPerLiter = getManualEfficiency(vehicle);
  return {
    kmPerLiter,
    source: kmPerLiter > 0 ? 'manual' : 'none',
    label: kmPerLiter > 0 ? 'Ingreso Manual' : 'Sin configurar'
  };
}


==================== ARCHIVO: src\utils\patentHelper.js ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: patentHelper.js
  PropÃ³sito: Validar mÃºltiples formatos de patentes chilenas (autos, motos, 
  remolques) y obtener automÃ¡ticamente el mes de PRT segÃºn el Ãºltimo dÃ­gito.
*/

export const PRT_CALENDAR = {
  '1': { monthName: 'Abril', monthNum: '04' },
  '2': { monthName: 'Mayo', monthNum: '05' },
  '3': { monthName: 'Junio', monthNum: '06' },
  '4': { monthName: 'Julio', monthNum: '07' },
  '5': { monthName: 'Agosto', monthNum: '08' },
  '6': { monthName: 'Septiembre', monthNum: '09' },
  '7': { monthName: 'Octubre', monthNum: '10' },
  '8': { monthName: 'Noviembre', monthNum: '11' },
  '9': { monthName: 'Enero', monthNum: '01' },
  '0': { monthName: 'Febrero', monthNum: '02' }
};

export function validateChileanPlate(plateStr = '') {
  const clean = plateStr.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g, '').toUpperCase();
  
  // 1. Auto Nuevo (2007+): 4 letras + 2 nÃºmeros (ej: BBCC10, BKN228)
  const autoNuevo = /^[B-DF-HJ-NP-TV-Z]{4}\d{2}$/;
  
  // 2. Auto Antiguo (1985-2007): 2 letras + 4 nÃºmeros (ej: AA1000)
  const autoAntiguo = /^[A-Z]{2}\d{4}$/;

  // 3. Moto Nuevo (2014+): 3 letras + 2 nÃºmeros (ej: ABC12) o 3 letras + 3 nÃºmeros
  const motoNuevo = /^[A-Z]{3}\d{2,3}$/;

  // 4. Moto Antiguo / Formatos Especiales: 2 letras + 3 nÃºmeros (ej: AB123)
  const motoAntiguo = /^[A-Z]{2}\d{3}$/;

  if (autoNuevo.test(clean)) {
    return { isValid: true, format: 'Auto/Camioneta (Nuevo: BBBB10)', cleanPlate: clean };
  }
  if (autoAntiguo.test(clean)) {
    return { isValid: true, format: 'Auto/Camioneta (Antiguo: AA1000)', cleanPlate: clean };
  }
  if (motoNuevo.test(clean)) {
    return { isValid: true, format: 'Motocicleta (Nuevo: ABC12)', cleanPlate: clean };
  }
  if (motoAntiguo.test(clean)) {
    return { isValid: true, format: 'Motocicleta (Antiguo: AB123)', cleanPlate: clean };
  }

  return { isValid: false, format: null, cleanPlate: clean };
}

export function getPrtInfoByPlate(plateStr = '') {
  const { isValid, format, cleanPlate } = validateChileanPlate(plateStr);
  if (!isValid) return null;

  // Extraer el Ãºltimo carÃ¡cter que sea un dÃ­gito numÃ©rico
  const matches = cleanPlate.match(/\d/g);
  if (!matches || matches.length === 0) return null;

  const lastDigit = matches[matches.length - 1];
  const prtData = PRT_CALENDAR[lastDigit];

  if (!prtData) return null;

  const today = new Date();
  let targetYear = today.getFullYear();
  const currentMonth = today.getMonth() + 1;
  const prtMonthInt = parseInt(prtData.monthNum, 10);

  if (prtMonthInt < currentMonth) {
    targetYear += 1;
  }

  const lastDayOfMonth = new Date(targetYear, prtMonthInt, 0).getDate();
  const expirationDate = `${targetYear}-${prtData.monthNum}-${String(lastDayOfMonth).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  return {
    lastDigit,
    monthName: prtData.monthName,
    format,
    expirationDate
  };
}


==================== ARCHIVO: src\utils\pdfGenerator.js ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: pdfGenerator.js
  PropÃ³sito: FunciÃ³n utilitaria para generar el Reporte Ejecutivo Integral en PDF
  incluyendo KPIs operativos, vehÃ­culos con rendimiento manual, guantera digital completa,
  desglose de gastos por categorÃ­a/registro e historial de cargas de combustible.
*/

const escapeHtml = (value) => {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  return String(value)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
};

const formatDateShort = (dateStr) => {
  if (!dateStr) return 'â€”';
  const parts = String(dateStr).split('-');
  if (parts.length === 3) {
    return `${parts[2]}/${parts[1]}/${parts[0]}`;
  }
  return escapeHtml(dateStr);
};

const getDocStatusBadge = (dateStr) => {
  if (!dateStr) {
    return `<span style="color: #64748b;">No configurada</span>`;
  }
  const diffDays = Math.ceil((new Date(dateStr) - new Date()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
  const formatted = formatDateShort(dateStr);

  if (diffDays < 0) {
    return `<strong style="color: #dc2626;">${formatted}</strong> <span style="background: #fee2e2; color: #b91c1c; padding: 1px 5px; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 10px; font-weight: bold;">VENCIDO</span>`;
  }
  if (diffDays <= 30) {
    return `<strong style="color: #d97706;">${formatted}</strong> <span style="background: #fef3c7; color: #b45309; padding: 1px 5px; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 10px; font-weight: bold;">${diffDays}d</span>`;
  }
  return `<strong style="color: #0f172a;">${formatted}</strong> <span style="background: #dcfce7; color: #15803d; padding: 1px 5px; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 10px; font-weight: bold;">AL DÃA</span>`;
};

export const generatePdfReport = ({
  vehicles = [],
  allVehicles = [],
  fuelRecords = [],
  expenses = [],
  totalFuelCost = 0,
  totalExpenses = 0,
  avgKm = '0.0',
  costPerKm = '0',
  dashVehicleFilter = 'all',
  indicators = { uf: 41226.39, dolar: 994.29 }
}) => {
  const fechaActual = new Date().toLocaleDateString('es-CL', {
    year: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    day: 'numeric'
  });

  const vehicleLookup = allVehicles.length > 0 ? allVehicles : vehicles;
  const resolveVehicleLabel = (vId) => {
    const found = vehicleLookup.find((v) => v.id === vId);
    if (!found) return 'VehÃ­culo no asignado';
    return `${escapeHtml(found.name)} (${escapeHtml(found.plate || 'S/P')})`;
  };

  const scopeLabel =
    dashVehicleFilter === 'all'
      ? `Flota Completa (${vehicles.length} vehÃ­culo${vehicles.length === 1 ? '' : 's'})`
      : vehicles[0]
        ? `${escapeHtml(vehicles[0].name)} â€¢ ${escapeHtml(vehicles[0].plate || 'Sin Patente')}`
        : 'VehÃ­culo Seleccionado';

  const totalGeneral = Number(totalFuelCost) + Number(totalExpenses);
  const totalInUf = indicators.uf > 0 ? (totalGeneral / indicators.uf).toFixed(2) : '0.00';
  const totalInUsd = indicators.dolar > 0 ? (totalGeneral / indicators.dolar).toFixed(2) : '0.00';
  const totalLiters = fuelRecords.reduce((acc, r) => acc + Number(r.liters || 0), 0);

  // 1. FILAS DE VEHÃCULOS
  let vehiclesRowsHtml = vehicles
    .map((v) => {
      const lastServiceText =
        v.last_service_date && Number(v.last_service_km) > 0
          ? `${formatDateShort(v.last_service_date)} (${Number(v.last_service_km).toLocaleString('es-CL')} km)`
          : 'Sin registro';

      const manualEff = Number(v.manual_efficiency || 0);
      const effText = manualEff > 0 ? `${manualEff.toFixed(1)} km/L` : 'No definido';

      const imgTag = v.image_url
        ? `<img src="${escapeHtml(v.image_url)}" style="width: 30px; height: 30px; border-radius: 4px; object-fit: cover; vertical-align: middle; margin-right: 8px;" />`
        : `<span style="font-size: 15px; margin-right: 8px;">ðŸš—</span>`;

      return `
        <tr>
          <td style="padding: 9px; border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5e1; font-weight: bold;">
            <div style="display: flex; align-items: center;">
              ${imgTag} <span>${escapeHtml(v.name || 'N/A')}</span>
            </div>
          </td>
          <td style="padding: 9px; border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5e1;">${escapeHtml(v.brand || 'N/A')} ${escapeHtml(v.model || '')} (${escapeHtml(v.year || '')})</td>
          <td style="padding: 9px; border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5e1; font-weight: bold; color: #0284c7;">${escapeHtml(v.plate || 'Sin Patente')}</td>
          <td style="padding: 9px; border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5e1; text-align: right;">${Number(v.currentKm || 0).toLocaleString('es-CL')} km</td>
          <td style="padding: 9px; border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5e1; text-align: right; font-weight: bold; color: ${manualEff > 0 ? '#16a34a' : '#64748b'};">${effText}</td>
          <td style="padding: 9px; border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5e1; font-size: 11px; color: #475569;">${lastServiceText}</td>
        </tr>
      `;
    })
    .join('');

  if (vehicles.length === 0) {
    vehiclesRowsHtml = `<tr><td colspan="6" style="padding: 16px; text-align: center; color: #64748b;">No hay vehÃ­culos registrados en este filtro.</td></tr>`;
  }

  // 2. FILAS DE GUANTERA DIGITAL (5 DOCUMENTOS)
  let documentsRowsHtml = vehicles
    .map((v) => {
      return `
        <tr style="background: #f1f5f9;">
          <td colspan="3" style="padding: 7px 10px; font-weight: bold; color: #0f172a; border-top: 1px solid #cbd5e1;">
            ${escapeHtml(v.name)} (${escapeHtml(v.plate || 'Sin Patente')})
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td style="padding: 7px 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; width: 33%;">RevisiÃ³n TÃ©cnica: ${getDocStatusBadge(v.revision_tecnica_expiration)}</td>
          <td style="padding: 7px 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; width: 34%;">Permiso CirculaciÃ³n: ${getDocStatusBadge(v.permiso_circulacion_expiration)}</td>
          <td style="padding: 7px 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; width: 33%;">SOAP: ${getDocStatusBadge(v.soap_expiration)}</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>

          <td colspan="2" style="padding: 7px 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5e1;">Licencia de Conducir: ${getDocStatusBadge(v.licencia_conducir_expiration)}</td>
        </tr>
      `;
    })
    .join('');

  if (vehicles.length === 0) {
    documentsRowsHtml = `<tr><td colspan="3" style="padding: 16px; text-align: center; color: #64748b;">Sin documentaciÃ³n para mostrar.</td></tr>`;
  }

  // 3. RESUMEN POR CATEGORÃA Y DETALLE DE GASTOS OPERATIVOS
  const categoryTotals = {};
  expenses.forEach((exp) => {
    const cat = exp.category || 'Otros';
    categoryTotals[cat] = (categoryTotals[cat] || 0) + Number(exp.amount || 0);
  });

  const categoryBadgesHtml = Object.entries(categoryTotals)
    .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
    .map(([cat, sum]) => {
      const pct = totalExpenses > 0 ? Math.round((sum / totalExpenses) * 100) : 0;

        </div>
      `;
    })
    .join('');

  let expensesRowsHtml = expenses
    .map((exp) => {
      const vId = exp.vehicle_id || exp.vehicleId;
      return `
        <tr>
          <td style="padding: 8px 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; white-space: nowrap;">${formatDateShort(exp.date)}</td>

          <td style="padding: 8px 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0;">
            <span style="background: #f1f5f9; border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 11px; font-weight: 600;">
              ${escapeHtml(exp.category || 'Otros')}
            </span>
          </td>

      `;
    })

  if (expenses.length === 0) {
    expensesRowsHtml = `<tr><td colspan="5" style="padding: 16px; text-align: center; color: #64748b;">No hay gastos operativos (TAG, peajes, mantenciones u otros) registrados.</td></tr>`;
  }

  // 4. HISTORIAL DETALLADO DE CARGAS DE COMBUSTIBLE
  const fuelTypeLabels = {
    '93': '93 Oct.',
    '95': '95 Oct.',
    '97': '97 Oct.',
    diesel: 'DiÃ©sel',
    kerosene: 'Kerosene'
  };

  let fuelRowsHtml = fuelRecords
    .map((rec) => {
      const vId = rec.vehicle_id || rec.vehicleId;
      const liters = Number(rec.liters || 0);
      const total = Number(rec.total || 0);
      const pricePerLiter = liters > 0 && total > 0 ? Math.round(total / liters) : 0;
      const odo = Number(rec.odometer || 0);
      const fuelLabel = fuelTypeLabels[String(rec.fuel_type || '95')] || escapeHtml(rec.fuel_type || '95');

      return `
        <tr>
          <td style="padding: 8px 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; white-space: nowrap;">${formatDateShort(rec.date)}</td>
          <td style="padding: 8px 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; font-weight: 600; color: #0284c7;">${resolveVehicleLabel(vId)}</td>

          <td style="padding: 8px 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0;">${fuelLabel}</td>
          <td style="padding: 8px 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid #e2e8f0; text-align: right;">${liters.toFixed(1)} L</td>
