            ${vehiclesRowsHtml}
          </tbody>
        </table>

        <div class="section-title">3. Estado de Guantera Digital (DocumentaciÃ³n Legal)</div>
        <table>
          <tbody>
            ${documentsRowsHtml}
          </tbody>
        </table>

        <div class="section-title">4. Detalle de Gastos Operativos (TAG, Peajes, Mantenciones y Otros)</div>
        ${categoryBadgesHtml ? `<div class="cat-grid">${categoryBadgesHtml}</div>` : ''}

          </thead>
          <tbody>
            ${expensesRowsHtml}
            ${
              expenses.length > 0
                ? `<tr style="background: #f8fafc; font-weight: bold;">
                    <td colspan="4" style="padding: 9px 10px; text-align: right; border-top: 2px solid #cbd5e1;">TOTAL GASTOS OPERATIVOS:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 9px 10px; text-align: right; color: #dc2626; border-top: 2px solid #cbd5e1;">$${Number(totalExpenses).toLocaleString('es-CL')}</td>
                  </tr>`
                : ''
            }
          </tbody>
        </table>

        <div class="section-title">5. Historial Detallado de Cargas de Combustible</div>
        <table>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Fecha</th>
              <th>VehÃ­culo</th>
              <th>EstaciÃ³n</th>
              <th>Tipo</th>
              <th style="text-align: right;">Litros</th>
              <th style="text-align: right;">Valor/L</th>
              <th style="text-align: right;">OdÃ³metro</th>
              <th style="text-align: right;">Total Pagado</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            ${fuelRowsHtml}
            ${
              fuelRecords.length > 0
                ? `<tr style="background: #f8fafc; font-weight: bold;">
                    <td colspan="4" style="padding: 9px 10px; text-align: right; border-top: 2px solid #cbd5e1;">TOTALES COMBUSTIBLE:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 9px 10px; text-align: right; border-top: 2px solid #cbd5e1;">${totalLiters.toFixed(1)} L</td>
                    <td colspan="2" style="border-top: 2px solid #cbd5e1;"></td>
                    <td style="padding: 9px 10px; text-align: right; color: #0284c7; border-top: 2px solid #cbd5e1;">$${Number(totalFuelCost).toLocaleString('es-CL')}</td>
                  </tr>`
                : ''
            }
          </tbody>
        </table>

        <div class="footer">
          Generado automÃ¡ticamente por Rendix Cloud â€¢ ${new Date().getFullYear()}
        </div>

        <script>
          window.onload = function() {
            setTimeout(function() {
              window.print();
            }, 250);
          };
        </script>
      </body>
    </html>
  `);
  printWindow.document.close();
};

import React from 'react';

export default function AlertsView({ vehicles = [], openServiceModal }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '24px', width: '100%', maxWidth: '1200px', margin: '0 auto', padding: '0 24px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}>
      
      <div style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid #334155', paddingBottom: '20px' }}>
        <h2 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '28px', color: '#f8fafc', fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: '-0.5px' }}>ðŸš¨ Panel de Alertas Mantenimiento y Legales</h2>
        <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 0', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px' }}>Monitorea el desgaste de fluidos/filtros y vencimientos de documentos en tiempo real.</p>
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(340px, 1fr))', gap: '24px' }}>
        {vehicles.map(v => {
          const currentKm = Number(v.currentKm || 0);
          const lastServiceKm = Number(v.last_service_km || 0);
          const intervalKm = 10000;
          const nextServiceKm = lastServiceKm > 0 ? lastServiceKm + intervalKm : Math.ceil((currentKm + 1) / intervalKm) * intervalKm;
          const remainingKm = nextServiceKm - currentKm;
          const progress = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, ((intervalKm - remainingKm) / intervalKm) * 100));

          const docs = [
            { name: 'SOAP', date: v.soap_expiration },
            { name: 'Permiso de CirculaciÃ³n', date: v.permiso_circulacion_expiration },
            { name: 'RevisiÃ³n TÃ©cnica', date: v.revision_tecnica_expiration },
            { name: 'Seguro Privado', date: v.seguro_privado_expiration },
            { name: 'Licencia de Conducir', date: v.licencia_conducir_expiration }
          ];

          return (
            <div key={v.id} style={{ background: '#1e293b', border: '1px solid #334155', borderRadius: '16px', overflow: 'hidden', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
              
              {/* Cabecera con foto del vehÃ­culo */}
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '14px', padding: '16px 20px', background: '#0f172a', borderBottom: '1px solid #334155' }}>
                {v.image_url ? (
                  <img 
                    src={v.image_url} 
                    alt={v.name} 
                    onError={(e) => { e.target.style.display = 'none'; }}
                    style={{ width: '48px', height: '48px', borderRadius: '10px', objectFit: 'cover', border: '1px solid #334155', flexShrink: 0 }} 
                  />
                ) : (
                  <div style={{ width: '48px', height: '48px', borderRadius: '10px', background: '#1e293b', border: '1px solid #334155', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: '22px', flexShrink: 0 }}>
                    ðŸš—
                  </div>
                )}
                <div>
                  <h4 style={{ margin: 0, color: '#f8fafc', fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: '800' }}>{v.name}</h4>
                  <span style={{ color: '#38bdf8', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '700' }}>{v.plate || 'Sin Patente'}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div style={{ padding: '20px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
                
                {/* Mantenimiento Aceite */}
                <div style={{ background: '#0f172a', padding: '14px', borderRadius: '12px', border: '1px solid #334155' }}>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: '8px', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '700' }}>
                    <span style={{ color: '#cbd5e1' }}>ðŸ”§ MantenciÃ³n Aceite/Filtros</span>
                    <span style={{ color: remainingKm <= 0 ? '#ef4444' : remainingKm <= 1500 ? '#eab308' : '#4ade80' }}>
                      {remainingKm <= 0 ? 'Â¡VENCIDO!' : `${remainingKm.toLocaleString('es-CL')} km restantes`}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ width: '100%', height: '8px', background: '#334155', borderRadius: '4px', overflow: 'hidden', marginBottom: '12px' }}>
                    <div style={{ width: `${progress}%`, height: '100%', background: remainingKm <= 0 ? '#ef4444' : remainingKm <= 1500 ? '#eab308' : '#22c55e', transition: 'width 0.3s' }} />
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => openServiceModal(v)}
                    style={{ width: '100%', background: '#3b82f6', color: '#fff', border: 'none', padding: '8px', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '800', cursor: 'pointer' }}
                  >
                    + Registrar MantenciÃ³n Hecha
                  </button>
                </div>

                {/* Documentos Legales */}
                <div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8', fontWeight: '700', marginBottom: '8px', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>Guantera Digital (Vencimientos)</div>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '6px' }}>
                    {docs.map((doc, idx) => {
                      if (!doc.date) return null;
                      const diffDays = Math.ceil((new Date(doc.date) - new Date()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
                      const isExpired = diffDays < 0;
                      const isWarning = diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 30;

                      return (
                        <div key={idx} style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: '12px', padding: '6px 10px', background: '#0f172a', borderRadius: '6px', border: '1px solid #334155' }}>
                          <span style={{ color: '#cbd5e1' }}>{doc.name}</span>
                          <span style={{ fontWeight: '700', color: isExpired ? '#ef4444' : isWarning ? '#eab308' : '#22c55e' }}>
                            {isExpired ? `Vencido (${doc.date})` : isWarning ? `Vence en ${diffDays} dÃ­as` : doc.date}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>

              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


==================== ARCHIVO: src\views\CneView.jsx ====================
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import L from 'leaflet';
import 'leaflet/dist/leaflet.css';

const COMMUNE_COORDS = {
  providencia: [-33.432, -70.612],
  santiago: [-33.448, -70.650],
  Ã±uÃ±oa: [-33.456, -70.603],
  'las condes': [-33.411, -70.578],
  macul: [-33.483, -70.598],
  maipÃº: [-33.510, -70.758],
  'la florida': [-33.523, -70.599],
  'san bernardo': [-33.593, -70.702],
  vitacura: [-33.395, -70.574],
  'puente alto': [-33.611, -70.575],
  peÃ±alolÃ©n: [-33.486, -70.542],
  'la reina': [-33.445, -70.544],
  recoleta: [-33.406, -70.641],
  independencia: [-33.416, -70.665],
  quilicura: [-33.361, -70.728],
  pudahuel: [-33.441, -70.749],
  'estaciÃ³n central': [-33.459, -70.695],
  'san miguel': [-33.498, -70.651]
};

function resolveStationCoords(station, index) {
  const lat = Number(station.lat || station.latitude || 0);
  const lng = Number(station.lng || station.longitude || 0);
  if (lat !== 0 && lng !== 0) return [lat, lng];

  const communeKey = (station.commune || '').toLowerCase();
  const base = Object.entries(COMMUNE_COORDS).find(([key]) => communeKey.includes(key))?.[1] || [-33.4489, -70.6693];
  const angle = (index * 137.5 * Math.PI) / 180;
  const radius = 0.004 + (index % 5) * 0.0015;
  return [base[0] + Math.cos(angle) * radius, base[1] + Math.sin(angle) * radius];
}

export default function CneView({ cneStations = [], isLoading = false, onReload }) {
  const [selectedFuel, setSelectedFuel] = useState('p95');
  const [selectedCommune, setSelectedCommune] = useState('todas');
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState('list'); // 'list' | 'map'

  const cneMapRef = useRef(null);
  const cneMapInstanceRef = useRef(null);
  const markersLayerRef = useRef(null);

  const communesList = ['todas', ...Array.from(new Set(cneStations.map(s => s.commune || 'Otra Comuna')))].sort();

  const filteredStations = cneStations
    .filter(station => {
      const matchesCommune = selectedCommune === 'todas' || station.commune === selectedCommune;
      const matchesSearch =
        (station.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
        (station.address || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
        (station.brand || '').toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase());
      const price = Number(station[selectedFuel] || 0);
      return matchesCommune && matchesSearch && price > 0;
    })
    .sort((a, b) => Number(a[selectedFuel] || 0) - Number(b[selectedFuel] || 0));

  const validPrices = filteredStations.map(s => Number(s[selectedFuel] || 0)).filter(p => p > 0);
  const minPrice = validPrices.length > 0 ? Math.min(...validPrices) : 0;
  const avgPrice = validPrices.length > 0 ? Math.round(validPrices.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / validPrices.length) : 0;

  const getFuelLabel = (key) => {
    switch (key) {
      case 'p93': return 'Gasolina 93';
      case 'p95': return 'Gasolina 95';
      case 'p97': return 'Gasolina 97';
      case 'diesel': return 'DiÃ©sel';
      case 'kerosene': return 'Kerosene';
      default: return 'Combustible';
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (viewMode !== 'map') {
      if (cneMapInstanceRef.current) {
        cneMapInstanceRef.current.remove();
        cneMapInstanceRef.current = null;
        markersLayerRef.current = null;
      }
      return;
    }

    if (!cneMapInstanceRef.current && cneMapRef.current) {
      const map = L.map(cneMapRef.current).setView([-33.4489, -70.6693], 11);
      L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
        attribution: '&copy; OpenStreetMap contributors'
      }).addTo(map);
      markersLayerRef.current = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);
      cneMapInstanceRef.current = map;
    }

    const map = cneMapInstanceRef.current;
    const layerGroup = markersLayerRef.current;
    if (!map || !layerGroup) return;

    layerGroup.clearLayers();
    const bounds = [];

    filteredStations.slice(0, 80).forEach((st, idx) => {
      const coords = resolveStationCoords(st, idx);
      const price = Number(st[selectedFuel] || 0);
      const isCheapest = price === minPrice;
      bounds.push(coords);

      const pinIcon = L.divIcon({
        className: 'cne-price-pin',
        html: `<div style="
          background:${isCheapest ? '#16a34a' : '#1e293b'};
          color:#ffffff;
          border:2px solid ${isCheapest ? '#86efac' : '#38bdf8'};
          border-radius:8px;
          padding:2px 6px;
          font-size:11px;
          font-weight:800;
          white-space:nowrap;
          box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.45);
        ">$${price.toLocaleString('es-CL')}</div>`,
        iconSize: [58, 24],
        iconAnchor: [29, 12]
      });

      const marker = L.marker(coords, { icon: pinIcon });
      marker.bindPopup(`
        <div style="min-width:170px;color:#0f172a;">
          <strong>${st.name}</strong><br/>
          <span style="font-size:12px;color:#475569;">${st.commune} â€¢ ${st.address || ''}</span><br/>
          <strong style="font-size:14px;color:${isCheapest ? '#16a34a' : '#2563eb'};">
            $${price.toLocaleString('es-CL')} / L
          </strong>
          ${isCheapest ? '<div style="margin-top:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#16a34a;">ðŸ·ï¸ MÃ¡s econÃ³mica del filtro</div>' : ''}
        </div>
      `);
      marker.addTo(layerGroup);
    });

    if (bounds.length > 0) {
      map.fitBounds(bounds, { padding: [40, 40], maxZoom: 14 });
    }
  }, [viewMode, filteredStations, selectedFuel, minPrice]);

  return (
    <div className="view-container" style={{ padding: '20px', color: '#f8fafc' }}>
      {/* Encabezado */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '20px', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '12px' }}>
        <div>
          <h2 style={{ margin: 0, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }}>
            <span>â›½</span> Red CNE - Bencinas en LÃ­nea RM
          </h2>
          <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 0', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px' }}>
            Precios de estaciones destacadas por comuna en la RegiÃ³n Metropolitana.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', background: '#1e293b', padding: '4px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #334155' }}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setViewMode('list')}
              style={{
                padding: '6px 12px',
                borderRadius: '6px',
                border: 'none',
                fontSize: '12px',
                fontWeight: '700',
                cursor: 'pointer',
                background: viewMode === 'list' ? '#3b82f6' : 'transparent',
                color: viewMode === 'list' ? '#ffffff' : '#94a3b8'
              }}
            >
              ðŸ“‹ Lista
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setViewMode('map')}
              style={{
                padding: '6px 12px',
                borderRadius: '6px',
                border: 'none',
                fontSize: '12px',
                fontWeight: '700',
                cursor: 'pointer',
                background: viewMode === 'map' ? '#3b82f6' : 'transparent',
                color: viewMode === 'map' ? '#ffffff' : '#94a3b8'
              }}
            >
              ðŸ—ºï¸ Mapa
            </button>
          </div>

          {onReload && (
            <button
              onClick={onReload}
              disabled={isLoading}
              style={{
                background: '#334155',
                color: '#ffffff',
                border: '1px solid #475569',
                padding: '8px 14px',
                borderRadius: '8px',
                fontWeight: '600',
                cursor: 'pointer',
                fontSize: '13px',
                opacity: isLoading ? 0.6 : 1
              }}
            >
              {isLoading ? 'ðŸ”„ Cargando...' : 'ðŸ”„ Actualizar Lista'}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Botones de selecciÃ³n de combustible */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', marginBottom: '20px', background: '#1e293b', padding: '8px', borderRadius: '10px', border: '1px solid #334155', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        {[
          { key: 'p93', label: 'Gasolina 93' },
          { key: 'p95', label: 'Gasolina 95' },
          { key: 'p97', label: 'Gasolina 97' },
          { key: 'diesel', label: 'DiÃ©sel' },
          { key: 'kerosene', label: 'Kerosene' }
        ].map(item => (
          <button
            key={item.key}
            onClick={() => setSelectedFuel(item.key)}
            style={{
              flex: 1,
              minWidth: '100px',
              padding: '10px',
              border: 'none',
              borderRadius: '6px',
              background: selectedFuel === item.key ? '#3b82f6' : 'transparent',
              color: selectedFuel === item.key ? '#ffffff' : '#94a3b8',
              fontWeight: selectedFuel === item.key ? '700' : '500',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              transition: 'all 0.2s'
            }}
          >
            {item.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Filtro por Comuna y Buscador */}
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 2fr', gap: '12px', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
        <div>
          <label style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8', display: 'block', marginBottom: '4px' }}>FILTRAR POR COMUNA</label>
          <select
            value={selectedCommune}
            onChange={(e) => setSelectedCommune(e.target.value)}
            style={{
              width: '100%',
              padding: '10px',
              borderRadius: '8px',
              background: '#0f172a',
              border: '1px solid #334155',
              color: '#fff',
              fontSize: '14px',
              boxSizing: 'border-box'
            }}
          >
            {communesList.map(c => (
              <option key={c} value={c}>
                {c === 'todas' ? 'Todas las Comunas' : c}

            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8', display: 'block', marginBottom: '4px' }}>BUSCAR ESTACIÃ“N O DIRECCIÃ“N</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Ej: Copec, Shell, Apoquindo, Matta..."
            value={searchTerm}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
            style={{
              width: '100%',
              padding: '10px',
              borderRadius: '8px',
              background: '#0f172a',
              border: '1px solid #334155',
              color: '#fff',

            }}

      {/* Cuadros comparativos de precios */}
      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '16px', marginBottom: '24px' }}>

          <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#4ade80', fontWeight: '600' }}>PRECIO MÃNIMO ENCONTRADO ({getFuelLabel(selectedFuel)})</span>
          <div style={{ fontSize: '28px', fontWeight: '800', color: '#22c55e', marginTop: '4px' }}>
            {minPrice > 0 ? `$${minPrice.toLocaleString('es-CL')} / L` : '---'}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ background: 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.1)', border: '1px solid rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.3)', borderRadius: '12px', padding: '16px', textAlign: 'center' }}>
          <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#60a5fa', fontWeight: '600' }}>PRECIO PROMEDIO ({getFuelLabel(selectedFuel)})</span>
          <div style={{ fontSize: '28px', fontWeight: '800', color: '#3b82f6', marginTop: '4px' }}>

          </div>
        </div>

      {viewMode === 'map' ? (

          <div ref={cneMapRef} style={{ height: '460px', width: '100%', borderRadius: '8px', overflow: 'hidden' }} />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ background: '#1e293b', borderRadius: '12px', border: '1px solid #334155', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
          <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', textAlign: 'left', color: '#f8fafc' }}>

              <tr style={{ background: '#0f172a', borderBottom: '1px solid #334155', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '12px' }}>

                <th style={{ padding: '14px' }}>DIRECCIÃ“N</th>

                </tr>
              ) : (
                filteredStations.map((st) => {
                  const currentPrice = Number(st[selectedFuel] || 0);
                  const isCheapest = currentPrice === minPrice;

                  return (
                    <tr key={st.id} style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid #334155', background: isCheapest ? 'rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.05)' : 'transparent' }}>
                      <td style={{ padding: '14px' }}>
                        <div style={{ fontWeight: '600', color: '#f8fafc', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                          {isCheapest && <span title="OpciÃ³n mÃ¡s econÃ³mica" style={{ fontSize: '14px' }}>ðŸ·ï¸</span>}
                          {st.name}
                        </div>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#94a3b8' }}>{st.brand || 'GenÃ©rica'}</span>
                      </td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '14px', color: '#38bdf8', fontWeight: '500' }}>{st.commune}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '14px', color: '#cbd5e1', fontSize: '13px' }}>{st.address}</td>
                      <td style={{ padding: '14px', textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: '700', fontSize: '16px', color: isCheapest ? '#4ade80' : '#f8fafc' }}>
                        ${currentPrice.toLocaleString('es-CL')} / L
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/*
  Nombre del archivo: DashboardView.jsx
  PropÃ³sito: Dashboard general de RendixApp completamente unificado y responsivo.
*/

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { generatePdfReport } from '../utils/pdfGenerator';

export default function DashboardView({
  vehicles = [],
  filteredVehicles,
  filteredFuels = [],
  filteredExpenses = [],
  totalFuelCost = 0,
  totalExpenses = 0,
  totalCargas = 0,
  avgKm = '0.0',
  costPerKm = '0',
  dashVehicleFilter = 'all',
  setDashVehicleFilter,
  setActiveTab,
  openAnalyticsModal
}) {
  const [indicators, setIndicators] = useState({ uf: 41226.39, dolar: 994.29 });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showGuide, setShowGuide] = useState(false);
  const [activeGuideTab, setActiveGuideTab] = useState('flow');

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([
      fetch('https://findic.cl/api/uf').then(res => res.json()).catch(() => null),
      fetch('https://findic.cl/api/dolar').then(res => res.json()).catch(() => null)
    ])
      .then(([ufData, dolarData]) => {
        const ufVal = ufData?.serie?.[0]?.valor || ufData?.valor || 41226.39;
        const dolarVal = dolarData?.serie?.[0]?.valor || dolarData?.valor || 994.29;

        setIndicators({
          uf: Number(ufVal),
          dolar: Number(dolarVal)
        });
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  const totalGeneral = totalFuelCost + totalExpenses;
  const scopedVehicles = filteredVehicles || vehicles;

  let urgentAlerts = 0;
  let warningAlerts = 0;

  vehicles.forEach(v => {
    const currentKm = Number(v.currentKm || 0);
    const lastServiceKm = Number(v.last_service_km || 0);
    const intervalKm = 10000;
    const nextServiceKm = lastServiceKm > 0 ? lastServiceKm + intervalKm : Math.ceil((currentKm + 1) / intervalKm) * intervalKm;
    const remainingKm = nextServiceKm - currentKm;
    
    if (lastServiceKm > 0 && remainingKm <= 0) urgentAlerts++;
    else if (lastServiceKm > 0 && remainingKm <= 1500) warningAlerts++;

    const docs = [v.soap_expiration, v.permiso_circulacion_expiration, v.revision_tecnica_expiration, v.seguro_privado_expiration, v.licencia_conducir_expiration];
    docs.forEach(dateStr => {
      if (dateStr) {
        const diffDays = Math.ceil((new Date(dateStr) - new Date()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
        if (diffDays < 0) urgentAlerts++;
        else if (diffDays <= 30) warningAlerts++;
      }
    });
  });

  const handleExportPdf = () => {
    generatePdfReport({
      vehicles: scopedVehicles,
      allVehicles: vehicles,
      fuelRecords: filteredFuels,
      expenses: filteredExpenses,
      totalFuelCost,
      totalExpenses,
      avgKm,
      costPerKm,
      dashVehicleFilter,
      indicators
    });
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px', width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}>
      
      {/* ðŸš¨ ALERTAS */}
      {(urgentAlerts > 0 || warningAlerts > 0) ? (
        <div 
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('alerts')}
          style={{ 
            background: urgentAlerts > 0 ? 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1)' : 'rgba(234, 179, 8, 0.1)', 
            border: `1px solid ${urgentAlerts > 0 ? 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3)' : 'rgba(234, 179, 8, 0.3)'}`, 
            borderRadius: '12px', padding: '14px 20px', display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', 
            alignItems: 'center', cursor: 'pointer', gap: '12px', width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box', flexWrap: 'wrap'
          }}
        >
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '24px' }}>{urgentAlerts > 0 ? 'ðŸš¨' : 'âš ï¸'}</span>
            <div>
              <h4 style={{ margin: 0, color: urgentAlerts > 0 ? '#ef4444' : '#eab308', fontSize: '15px', fontWeight: '800' }}>
                AtenciÃ³n Requerida en tu Flota
              </h4>
              <p style={{ margin: '2px 0 0 0', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                {urgentAlerts > 0 ? `${urgentAlerts} alerta(s) urgente(s). ` : ''}
                {warningAlerts > 0 ? `${warningAlerts} estado(s) prÃ³ximo(s) a vencer.` : ''}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '700', background: 'var(--bg-secondary)', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)', padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: '6px' }}>
            Ir a Alertas â†’
          </span>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ background: 'rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.1)', border: '1px solid rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.3)', borderRadius: '12px', padding: '14px 20px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px', width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}>
          <span style={{ fontSize: '22px' }}>âœ…</span>
          <div>
            <h4 style={{ margin: 0, color: '#22c55e', fontSize: '15px', fontWeight: '800' }}>Flota al DÃ­a</h4>
            <p style={{ margin: '2px 0 0 0', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: '12px' }}>Todos los mantenimientos y documentos estÃ¡n en regla.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* TOPBAR */}
      <div style={{ 
        display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', 
        gap: '16px', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border-color)', paddingBottom: '16px', width: '100%',
        flexWrap: 'wrap'
      }}>
        <div>
          <h2 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '24px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: '-0.5px' }}>Dashboard General</h2>
          <p style={{ margin: '2px 0 0 0', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: '13px' }}>Resumen financiero y rendimiento operativo de tus vehÃ­culos en tiempo real.</p>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', background: 'var(--bg-card)', padding: '6px 10px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)' }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginRight: '6px' }}>ðŸš—</span>
            <select
              value={dashVehicleFilter}
              onChange={(e) => setDashVehicleFilter(e.target.value)}
              style={{ background: 'transparent', color: 'var(--text-primary)', border: 'none', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '700', outline: 'none', cursor: 'pointer' }}
            >
              <option value="all" style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card)' }}>Todos los vehÃ­culos ({vehicles.length})</option>
              {vehicles.map(v => (
                <option key={v.id} value={v.id} style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card)' }}>
                  {v.name} ({v.brand} {v.model})
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={openAnalyticsModal}
            style={{
              background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #8b5cf6, #6d28d9)', color: '#ffffff', border: 'none',
              padding: '8px 12px', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '800', cursor: 'pointer',
              boxShadow: '0 4px 6px -1px rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.3)'
            }}
          >
            ðŸ“ˆ AnalÃ­tica
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => setShowGuide(!showGuide)}
            style={{
              background: showGuide ? '#38bdf8' : 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.15)',
              color: showGuide ? '#0f172a' : '#38bdf8',
              border: '1px solid #38bdf8', padding: '8px 14px', borderRadius: '8px',
              fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '800', cursor: 'pointer'
            }}
          >
            ðŸ“– {showGuide ? 'Ocultar GuÃ­a' : 'Â¿CÃ³mo Usar?'}
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={handleExportPdf}
            style={{
              background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #3b82f6, #1d4ed8)', color: '#ffffff', border: 'none',
              padding: '8px 14px', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '800', cursor: 'pointer',
              boxShadow: '0 4px 6px -1px rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.3)'
            }}
          >
            ðŸ–¨ï¸ PDF
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* GUÃA DE USO */}
      {showGuide && (
        <div style={{
          background: 'var(--bg-card)', border: '1px solid #38bdf8', borderRadius: '16px',
          padding: '20px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px',
          boxShadow: '0 20px 25px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2)', width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box'
        }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border-color)', paddingBottom: '10px', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '8px' }}>
            <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '16px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontWeight: '900' }}>
              ðŸ“– Manual Operativo â€¢ RendixApp
            </h3>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', background: 'var(--bg-secondary)', color: '#38bdf8', padding: '4px 8px', borderRadius: '6px', fontWeight: '800', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)' }}>
              Duoc UC Capstone
            </span>
          </div>

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border-color)', paddingBottom: '8px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <button onClick={() => setActiveGuideTab('flow')} style={{ background: activeGuideTab === 'flow' ? '#38bdf8' : 'var(--bg-secondary)', color: activeGuideTab === 'flow' ? '#0f172a' : 'var(--text-secondary)', border: 'none', padding: '6px 10px', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '800', cursor: 'pointer' }}>1. Flujo</button>
            <button onClick={() => setActiveGuideTab('formulas')} style={{ background: activeGuideTab === 'formulas' ? '#38bdf8' : 'var(--bg-secondary)', color: activeGuideTab === 'formulas' ? '#0f172a' : 'var(--text-secondary)', border: 'none', padding: '6px 10px', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '800', cursor: 'pointer' }}>2. FÃ³rmulas</button>
            <button onClick={() => setActiveGuideTab('trip')} style={{ background: activeGuideTab === 'trip' ? '#38bdf8' : 'var(--bg-secondary)', color: activeGuideTab === 'trip' ? '#0f172a' : 'var(--text-secondary)', border: 'none', padding: '6px 10px', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '800', cursor: 'pointer' }}>3. Fletes</button>
            <button onClick={() => setActiveGuideTab('alerts')} style={{ background: activeGuideTab === 'alerts' ? '#38bdf8' : 'var(--bg-secondary)', color: activeGuideTab === 'alerts' ? '#0f172a' : 'var(--text-secondary)', border: 'none', padding: '6px 10px', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '800', cursor: 'pointer' }}>4. Alertas</button>
          </div>

          {activeGuideTab === 'flow' && (
            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(220px, 1fr))', gap: '10px' }}>
              <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card-inner)', padding: '12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)' }}>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: '800', color: '#38bdf8', marginBottom: '4px' }}>1. Mis VehÃ­culos</div>
                <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Ingresa tu vehÃ­culo con su patente (PRT automÃ¡tica) y su rendimiento manual en km/L.</p>
              </div>
              <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card-inner)', padding: '12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)' }}>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: '800', color: '#4ade80', marginBottom: '4px' }}>2. Cargas Combustible</div>
                <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Registra tus litros y monto pagado (el odÃ³metro es opcional) para llevar el historial y precio por litro.</p>
              </div>
              <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card-inner)', padding: '12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)' }}>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: '800', color: '#fca5a5', marginBottom: '4px' }}>3. Gastos y TAG</div>
                <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Registra peajes y mantenciones para calcular el Costo Real por KilÃ³metro ($/km).</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {activeGuideTab === 'formulas' && (
            <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(220px, 1fr))', gap: '10px' }}>
              <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card-inner)', padding: '12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)' }}>
                <h4 style={{ margin: '0 0 4px 0', fontSize: '12px', color: '#4ade80', fontWeight: '800' }}>Rendimiento Manual (km/L)</h4>
                <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-secondary)', padding: '6px', borderRadius: '4px', fontSize: '10px', color: '#38bdf8', fontWeight: '700', textAlign: 'center' }}>
                  Promedio de Rendimiento Configurado por VehÃ­culo
                </div>
              </div>
              <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card-inner)', padding: '12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)' }}>
                <h4 style={{ margin: '0 0 4px 0', fontSize: '12px', color: '#06b6d4', fontWeight: '800' }}>Costo Real / Km (TCO)</h4>
                <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-secondary)', padding: '6px', borderRadius: '4px', fontSize: '10px', color: '#06b6d4', fontWeight: '700', textAlign: 'center' }}>
                  (Bencina + Gastos) / (Litros Ã— km/L)
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {activeGuideTab === 'trip' && (
            <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card-inner)', padding: '12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)' }}>
              <h4 style={{ margin: '0 0 4px 0', fontSize: '13px', color: '#10b981', fontWeight: '800' }}>ðŸ—ºï¸ Cotizador de Fletes</h4>
              <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Marca origen y destino en el mapa para trazar la ruta por calles y obtener tu tarifa de cobro ideal con margen.</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {activeGuideTab === 'alerts' && (
            <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card-inner)', padding: '12px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)' }}>
              <h4 style={{ margin: '0 0 4px 0', fontSize: '13px', color: '#ef4444', fontWeight: '800' }}>ðŸ“ Guantera Digital</h4>
              <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Controla los vencimientos de SOAP, RevisiÃ³n TÃ©cnica y mantenciones preventivas cada 10.000 km.</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* WIDGET FINDIC API */}
      <div style={{
        background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.15), var(--bg-card))',
        border: '1px solid var(--border-color)', borderRadius: '12px', padding: '16px 20px',
        display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '16px', boxSizing: 'border-box'
      }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
          <div style={{ background: 'rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.25)', padding: '8px 10px', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '20px' }}>ðŸ“Š</div>
          <div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px' }}>
              <h4 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '14px', color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontWeight: '800' }}>Findic API</h4>
              <span style={{ background: '#22c55e', color: '#0f172a', fontSize: '8px', fontWeight: '800', padding: '2px 4px', borderRadius: '4px' }}>EN VIVO</span>
            </div>
            <p style={{ margin: '2px 0 0 0', fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>ConversiÃ³n automÃ¡tica de UF y DÃ³lar.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '16px', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          <div style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '9px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontWeight: '700', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>UF</span>
            <div style={{ fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: '950', color: '#38bdf8' }}>{loading ? '...' : `$${Number(indicators.uf).toLocaleString('es-CL', { minimumFractionDigits: 2 })}`}</div>
          </div>
          <div style={{ borderLeft: '1px solid var(--border-color)', paddingLeft: '16px', textAlign: 'right' }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '9px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontWeight: '700', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>DÃ³lar</span>
            <div style={{ fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: '950', color: '#4ade80' }}>{loading ? '...' : `$${Number(indicators.dolar).toLocaleString('es-CL', { minimumFractionDigits: 2 })}`}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* GRID KPI */}
      <div style={{ 
        display: 'grid', 
        gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr))', 
        gap: '12px', 
        width: '100%',
        boxSizing: 'border-box'
      }}>
        <div style={{ position: 'relative', padding: '16px', background: 'var(--bg-card)', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)', borderRadius: '12px' }}>

          <div style={{ color: '#38bdf8', fontSize: '20px', fontWeight: '900', marginTop: '6px' }}>${Number(totalFuelCost).toLocaleString('es-CL')}</div>
          <span style={{ position: 'absolute', right: '12px', bottom: '12px', fontSize: '18px' }}>â›½</span>
        </div>

        <div style={{ position: 'relative', padding: '16px', background: 'var(--bg-card)', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)', borderRadius: '12px' }}>
          <div style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '700', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>Rendimiento Prom.</div>
          <div style={{ color: '#4ade80', fontSize: '20px', fontWeight: '900', marginTop: '6px' }}>{avgKm} <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '500', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>km/l</span></div>
          <span style={{ position: 'absolute', right: '12px', bottom: '12px', fontSize: '18px' }}>ðŸ›¤ï¸</span>
        </div>

        <div style={{ position: 'relative', padding: '16px', background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, var(--bg-card), rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.15))', border: '1px solid rgba(6, 182, 212, 0.4)', borderRadius: '12px' }}>

          <div style={{ color: '#06b6d4', fontSize: '20px', fontWeight: '900', marginTop: '6px' }}>${costPerKm} <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '500', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>/ km</span></div>
          <span style={{ position: 'absolute', right: '12px', bottom: '12px', fontSize: '18px' }}>ðŸ’¸</span>
        </div>

        <div style={{ position: 'relative', padding: '16px', background: 'var(--bg-card)', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)', borderRadius: '12px' }}>
          <div style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '700', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>Gastos Operativos</div>
          <div style={{ color: '#fca5a5', fontSize: '20px', fontWeight: '900', marginTop: '6px' }}>${Number(totalExpenses).toLocaleString('es-CL')}</div>
          <span style={{ position: 'absolute', right: '12px', bottom: '12px', fontSize: '18px' }}>ðŸ› ï¸</span>
        </div>

        <div style={{ position: 'relative', padding: '16px', background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, var(--bg-card), rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.15))', border: '1px solid rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.4)', borderRadius: '12px' }}>
          <div style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '700', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>Gasto UF y DÃ³lares</div>
          <div style={{ color: '#818cf8', fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: '900', marginTop: '6px' }}>
            {indicators.uf > 0 ? (totalGeneral / indicators.uf).toFixed(2) : '0.00'} UF 
            <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', display: 'block', marginTop: '2px' }}>(US${indicators.dolar > 0 ? (totalGeneral / indicators.dolar).toFixed(2) : '0.00'})</span>
          </div>
          <span style={{ position: 'absolute', right: '12px', bottom: '12px', fontSize: '18px' }}>ðŸ“Š</span>
        </div>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}


==================== ARCHIVO: src\views\DocumentsView.jsx ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: DocumentsView.jsx
  PropÃ³sito: Billetera Digital de documentos de la flota con persistencia y eliminaciÃ³n de archivos en Supabase.
*/

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '../supabaseClient';

export default function DocumentsView({ vehicles, onSaveDocuments }) {
  const [selectedVehicleId, setSelectedVehicleId] = useState(vehicles[0]?.id || '');
  const selectedVehicle = vehicles.find(v => v.id === selectedVehicleId) || vehicles[0];

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    soap_expiration: selectedVehicle?.soap_expiration || '',
    permiso_circulacion_expiration: selectedVehicle?.permiso_circulacion_expiration || '',
    revision_tecnica_expiration: selectedVehicle?.revision_tecnica_expiration || '',
    seguro_privado_expiration: selectedVehicle?.seguro_privado_expiration || '',
    licencia_conducir_expiration: selectedVehicle?.licencia_conducir_expiration || '',
    
    soap_file: selectedVehicle?.soap_file || '',
    permiso_file: selectedVehicle?.permiso_file || '',
    revision_file: selectedVehicle?.revision_file || '',
    seguro_file: selectedVehicle?.seguro_file || '',
    licencia_file: selectedVehicle?.licencia_file || ''
  });

  const [uploadingField, setUploadingField] = useState(null);

  // Sincronizar el estado del formulario cuando cambia el vehÃ­culo seleccionado
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedVehicle) {
      setFormData({
        soap_expiration: selectedVehicle.soap_expiration || '',
        permiso_circulacion_expiration: selectedVehicle.permiso_circulacion_expiration || '',
        revision_tecnica_expiration: selectedVehicle.revision_tecnica_expiration || '',
        seguro_privado_expiration: selectedVehicle.seguro_privado_expiration || '',
        licencia_conducir_expiration: selectedVehicle.licencia_conducir_expiration || '',
        
        soap_file: selectedVehicle?.soap_file || '',
        permiso_file: selectedVehicle?.permiso_file || '',
        revision_file: selectedVehicle?.revision_file || '',
        seguro_file: selectedVehicle?.seguro_file || '',
        licencia_file: selectedVehicle?.licencia_file || ''
      });
    }
  }, [selectedVehicleId, vehicles]);

  const handleChange = (field, value) => {
    setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [field]: value }));
  };

  const handleFileUpload = async (fileKey, e) => {
    const file = e.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setUploadingField(fileKey);

    try {
      const fileExt = file.name.split('.').pop();
      const fileName = `${selectedVehicle.id}_${fileKey}_${Date.now()}.${fileExt}`;
      const filePath = `documents/${fileName}`;

      // Subir a Supabase Storage
      const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
        .from('vehicle-docs')
        .upload(filePath, file);

      if (uploadError) {
        console.error('Error al subir documento:', uploadError);
        alert(`Error al subir: ${uploadError.message}`);
      } else {
        const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabase.storage
          .from('vehicle-docs')
          .getPublicUrl(filePath);

        // Actualizar el estado con la URL pÃºblica generada
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [fileKey]: publicUrl }));
        alert(`âœ¨ Archivo adjuntado correctamente.`);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('ExcepciÃ³n al subir archivo:', err);
    } finally {
      setUploadingField(null);
    }
  };

  // NUEVA FUNCIÃ“N: Eliminar documento del estado
  const handleRemoveFile = (fileKey) => {
    if (window.confirm('Â¿Deseas quitar este documento? Recuerda presionar "Guardar Billetera Digital" para confirmar el cambio.')) {
      setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [fileKey]: '' }));
    }
  };

  const handleSave = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!selectedVehicle) return;

    // Convertir campos vacÃ­os a null para evitar errores en Supabase
    const cleanedData = {
      soap_expiration: formData.soap_expiration || null,
      permiso_circulacion_expiration: formData.permiso_circulacion_expiration || null,
      revision_tecnica_expiration: formData.revision_tecnica_expiration || null,
      seguro_privado_expiration: formData.seguro_privado_expiration || null,
      licencia_conducir_expiration: formData.licencia_conducir_expiration || null,
      
      soap_file: formData.soap_file || null,
      permiso_file: formData.permiso_file || null,
      revision_file: formData.revision_file || null,
      seguro_file: formData.seguro_file || null,
      licencia_file: formData.licencia_file || null
    };

    onSaveDocuments(selectedVehicle.id, cleanedData);
  };

  if (!vehicles || vehicles.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '40px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>
        <h2>ðŸ“ Billetera Digital</h2>
        <p>No hay vehÃ­culos registrados en tu flota. Agrega uno primero en "Mis VehÃ­culos".</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const docFields = [
    { key: 'soap_expiration', fileKey: 'soap_file', label: 'SOAP (Seguro Obligatorio)', icon: 'ðŸ›¡ï¸' },
    { key: 'permiso_circulacion_expiration', fileKey: 'permiso_file', label: 'Permiso de CirculaciÃ³n', icon: 'ðŸ“„' },
    { key: 'revision_tecnica_expiration', fileKey: 'revision_file', label: 'RevisiÃ³n TÃ©cnica / Gases', icon: 'ðŸ”' },
    { key: 'seguro_privado_expiration', fileKey: 'seguro_file', label: 'Seguro Privado / Cobertura', icon: 'ðŸ’¼' },
    { key: 'licencia_conducir_expiration', fileKey: 'licencia_file', label: 'Licencia de Conducir', icon: 'ðŸªª' }
  ];

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px', width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}>
      <div>
        <h2 style={{ margin: '0 0 6px 0', fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: '950', color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>
          ðŸ“ Billetera Digital de Documentos
        </h2>
        <p style={{ margin: 0, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: '14px' }}>
          Guarda los documentos oficiales y controla sus fechas de expiraciÃ³n en un solo lugar seguro.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* SELECTOR DE VEHÃCULO */}
      <div style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card)', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)', borderRadius: '16px', padding: '16px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px' }}>
        <label style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '700', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Seleccionar VehÃ­culo a Consultar:</label>
        <select 
          value={selectedVehicleId} 
          onChange={(e) => setSelectedVehicleId(e.target.value)}
          style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card-inner)', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)', color: 'var(--text-primary)', padding: '10px 12px', borderRadius: '10px', fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: '800' }}
        >
          {vehicles.map(v => (
            <option key={v.id} value={v.id}>
              {v.name} - Patente: {v.plate || 'S/P'}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>

      {/* TARJETA DE VEHÃCULO DESTACADA */}
      {selectedVehicle && (
        <div style={{ 
          background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, var(--bg-card) 0%, var(--bg-card-inner) 100%)', 
          border: '1px solid var(--border-color)', 
          borderRadius: '16px', 
          padding: '24px', 
          display: 'flex', 
          alignItems: 'center', 
          gap: '24px', 
          flexWrap: 'wrap',
          boxShadow: '0 10px 15px -3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2)'
        }}>
          {selectedVehicle.image_url ? (
            <img 
              src={selectedVehicle.image_url} 
              alt={selectedVehicle.name} 
              style={{ width: '160px', height: '110px', objectFit: 'cover', borderRadius: '12px', border: '2px solid var(--border-color)', boxShadow: '0 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.3)' }}
            />
          ) : (
            <div style={{ width: '160px', height: '110px', background: 'var(--bg-card-inner)', borderRadius: '12px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: '42px', border: '2px solid var(--border-color)' }}>
              ðŸš˜
            </div>
          )}

          <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '6px', minWidth: '220px' }}>
            <span style={{ background: 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.1)', color: '#38bdf8', padding: '3px 10px', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '900', textTransform: 'uppercase', border: '1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.3)', width: 'fit-content' }}>
              {selectedVehicle.brand || 'VehÃ­culo'} {selectedVehicle.model || ''} ({selectedVehicle.year || '202X'})
            </span>
            
            <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '26px', fontWeight: '950', color: 'var(--text-primary)', letterSpacing: '-0.5px' }}>
              {selectedVehicle.name}
            </h3>
            
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '16px', fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontWeight: '700', marginTop: '2px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
              <span>Patente: <strong style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)', background: 'var(--bg-card)', padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: '4px', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)' }}>{selectedVehicle.plate || 'SIN PATENTE'}</strong></span>
              <span>Kilometraje Actual: <strong style={{ color: '#38bdf8' }}>{Number(selectedVehicle.currentKm || 0).toLocaleString('es-CL')} km</strong></span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* FORMULARIO DE DOCUMENTOS Y ARCHIVOS */}
      <form onSubmit={handleSave} style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card)', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)', borderRadius: '16px', padding: '20px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
        <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: '900', color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>
          Documentos Guardados en la Billetera
        </h3>

        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(300px, 1fr))', gap: '16px' }}>
          {docFields.map(doc => {
            const dateVal = formData[doc.key] || '';
            const fileVal = formData[doc.fileKey] || '';
            const isNear = dateVal && Math.ceil((new Date(dateVal) - new Date()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) <= 30;

            return (
              <div key={doc.key} style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card-inner)', border: `1px solid ${isNear ? '#ef4444' : 'var(--border-color)'}`, borderRadius: '12px', padding: '14px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '10px' }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: '800', color: 'var(--text-primary)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px' }}>
                    <span>{doc.icon}</span> {doc.label}
                  </span>
                  {isNear && (
                    <span style={{ background: '#ef4444', color: '#fff', fontSize: '10px', fontWeight: '900', padding: '2px 6px', borderRadius: '6px' }}>
                      Por vencer
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* FECHA DE VENCIMIENTO */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '4px' }}>
                  <label style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontWeight: '700' }}>Fecha de Vencimiento:</label>
                  <input 
                    type="date"
                    value={dateVal}
                    onChange={(e) => handleChange(doc.key, e.target.value)}
                    style={{ background: 'var(--bg-card)', border: '1px solid var(--border-color)', color: 'var(--text-primary)', padding: '8px 10px', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '13px', width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
                  />
                </div>

                {/* VISUALIZAR, CAMBIAR Y ELIMINAR ARCHIVO */}
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '4px', marginTop: '2px' }}>
                  <label style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontWeight: '700' }}>Documento en Billetera:</label>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                    {fileVal ? (
                      <a 
                        href={fileVal.startsWith('http') ? fileVal : '#'} 
                        target="_blank" 
                        rel="noreferrer"
                        style={{ 
                          background: 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.1)', 
                          border: '1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.3)', 
                          color: '#38bdf8', 
                          padding: '6px 10px', 
                          borderRadius: '8px', 
                          fontSize: '12px', 
                          fontWeight: '800', 
                          textDecoration: 'none',
                          display: 'flex',
                          alignItems: 'center',
                          gap: '6px',
                          flex: 1,
                          overflow: 'hidden',
                          textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                          whiteSpace: 'nowrap'
                        }}
                        title="Ver / Descargar documento"
                      >
                        <span>ðŸ“¥</span> 
                        <span style={{ overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis' }}>Ver Documento Adjunto</span>
                      </a>
                    ) : (
                      <span style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontStyle: 'italic' }}>
                        Sin archivo adjunto
                      </span>
                    )}

                    {/* BOTONES DE ACCIÃ“N: Eliminar y Adjuntar/Cambiar */}
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                      {fileVal && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleRemoveFile(doc.fileKey)}
                          style={{ background: '#ef4444', border: 'none', color: '#fff', padding: '6px 10px', borderRadius: '8px', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: '12px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}
                          title="Quitar documento"
                        >
                          ðŸ—‘ï¸
                        </button>
                      )}
                      
                      <label style={{ background: '#3b82f6', color: '#fff', padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: '8px', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '700', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', margin: 0 }}>
                        {uploadingField === doc.fileKey ? 'Subiendo...' : (fileVal ? 'ðŸ”„ Cambiar' : 'ðŸ“Ž Adjuntar')}
                        <input 
                          type="file" 
                          accept="image/*,.pdf" 
                          onChange={(e) => handleFileUpload(doc.fileKey, e)} 
                          style={{ display: 'none' }} 
                        />
                      </label>
                    </div>

                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', marginTop: '10px' }}>
          <button 
            type="submit" 
            style={{ background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #3b82f6, #1d4ed8)', border: 'none', color: '#fff', padding: '12px 24px', borderRadius: '10px', fontWeight: '900', fontSize: '14px', cursor: 'pointer', boxShadow: '0 4px 6px -1px rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.3)' }}
          >
            ðŸ’¾ Guardar Billetera Digital
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';

export default function ExpensesView({ expenses, vehicles, openModal, onDeleteExpense }) {
  // FunciÃ³n auxiliar para obtener datos e imagen del vehÃ­culo asociado
  const getVehicleInfo = (vehicleId) => {
    const found = vehicles.find(v => v.id === vehicleId);
    return found || { name: 'VehÃ­culo Desconocido', plate: '---', image_url: '' };
  };

  return (
    <div className="view-container">
      {/* Encabezado de la Vista */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
        <div>
          <h2 style={{ margin: 0, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: '#f8fafc' }}>
            <span>ðŸ’¸</span> Gastos Operativos y TAG
          </h2>
          <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 0', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px' }}>
            Registra peajes, repuestos, estacionamientos y mantenimientos adicionales.
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={openModal}
          style={{
            background: '#2563eb',
            color: '#ffffff',
            border: 'none',
            padding: '10px 18px',
            borderRadius: '8px',
            fontWeight: '600',
            cursor: 'pointer',
            fontSize: '14px'
          }}
        >
          + Registrar Nuevo Gasto
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Tabla de Gastos */}
      <div style={{ background: '#1e293b', borderRadius: '12px', border: '1px solid #334155', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
        <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', textAlign: 'left', color: '#f8fafc' }}>
          <thead>
            <tr style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid #334155', background: '#0f172a', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '12px', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>
              <th style={{ padding: '16px' }}>FECHA</th>
              <th style={{ padding: '16px' }}>VEHÃCULO</th>
              <th style={{ padding: '16px' }}>CATEGORÃA</th>
              <th style={{ padding: '16px' }}>DESCRIPCIÃ“N</th>
              <th style={{ padding: '16px', textAlign: 'right' }}>MONTO</th>
              <th style={{ padding: '16px', textAlign: 'center' }}>ACCIONES</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {expenses.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan="6" style={{ padding: '32px', textAlign: 'center', color: '#64748b' }}>
                  No hay gastos registrados aÃºn.
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              expenses.map((expense) => {
                const vehicleId = expense.vehicle_id || expense.vehicleId;
                const vehicle = getVehicleInfo(vehicleId);

                return (
                  <tr key={expense.id} style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid #334155' }}>
                    <td style={{ padding: '16px', fontWeight: '500' }}>{expense.date}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '16px' }}>
                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }}>
                        {vehicle.image_url ? (
                          <img
                            src={vehicle.image_url}
                            alt={vehicle.name}
                            style={{ width: '36px', height: '36px', borderRadius: '50%', objectFit: 'cover' }}
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <div style={{ width: '36px', height: '36px', borderRadius: '50%', background: '#334155', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: '16px' }}>
                            ðŸš˜
                          </div>
                        )}
                        <div>
                          <div style={{ fontWeight: '600', color: '#38bdf8' }}>{vehicle.name}</div>
                          <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#64748b' }}>{vehicle.plate}</div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '16px' }}>
                      <span style={{
                        background: '#334155',
                        color: '#e2e8f0',
                        padding: '4px 10px',
                        borderRadius: '6px',
                        fontSize: '12px',
                        fontWeight: '500'
                      }}>
                        {expense.category || 'General'}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '16px', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px' }}>
                      {expense.description || '-'}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '16px', textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: '700', color: '#f43f5e' }}>
                      ${Number(expense.amount || 0).toLocaleString('es-CL')}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '16px', textAlign: 'center' }}>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => onDeleteExpense && onDeleteExpense(expense.id)}
                        title="Eliminar gasto"
                        style={{
                          background: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1)',
                          border: '1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3)',
                          color: '#ef4444',
                          borderRadius: '6px',
                          padding: '6px 10px',
                          cursor: 'pointer',
                          fontSize: '14px',
                          transition: 'background 0.2s'
                        }}
                      >
                        ðŸ—‘ï¸
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


==================== ARCHIVO: src\views\FuelView.jsx ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: FuelView.jsx
  PropÃ³sito: Historial de cargas de combustible con avatar del vehÃ­culo,
  referencia de precios en vivo desde API CNE y eliminaciÃ³n.
*/

import React, { useState } from 'react';

export default function FuelView({
  fuelRecords = [],
  vehicles = [],
  cneStations = [],
  isCneLoading = false,
  openModal,
  onDeleteFuel
}) {
  const [refFuelType, setRefFuelType] = useState('p95');

  const fuelLabels = {
    p93: '93 Oct.',
    p95: '95 Oct.',
    p97: '97 Oct.',
    diesel: 'DiÃ©sel'
  };

  const validStations = cneStations.filter((s) => Number(s[refFuelType]) > 0);
  const cheapestStation =
    validStations.length > 0
      ? validStations.reduce(
          (min, curr) => (Number(curr[refFuelType]) < Number(min[refFuelType]) ? curr : min),
          validStations[0]
        )
      : null;

  const renderVehicleCell = (vehicleId) => {
    const v = vehicles.find(item => item.id === vehicleId);
    if (!v) return <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8' }}>VehÃ­culo no encontrado</span>;

    return (
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
        {v.image_url ? (
          <img 
            src={v.image_url} 
            alt={v.name} 
            onError={(e) => { e.target.style.display = 'none'; }}
            style={{ width: '36px', height: '36px', borderRadius: '50%', objectFit: 'cover', border: '1px solid #334155', flexShrink: 0 }} 
          />
        ) : (
          <div style={{ width: '36px', height: '36px', borderRadius: '50%', background: '#0f172a', border: '1px solid #334155', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: '16px', flexShrink: 0 }}>
            ðŸš—
          </div>
        )}
        <div>
          <div style={{ color: '#38bdf8', fontWeight: '800', fontSize: '13px' }}>{v.name}</div>
          <div style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '600' }}>{v.plate || 'Sin Patente'}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '24px', width: '100%', maxWidth: '1200px', margin: '0 auto', padding: '0 24px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}>
      
      {/* Encabezado */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '16px', borderBottom: '1px solid #334155', paddingBottom: '20px' }}>
        <div>
          <h2 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '28px', color: '#f8fafc', fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: '-0.5px' }}>â›½ Historial de Cargas de Bencina</h2>
          <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 0', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px' }}>Controla tus litros cargados y el gasto histÃ³rico por vehÃ­culo.</p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={openModal}
          style={{
            background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #3b82f6, #1d4ed8)',
            color: '#ffffff',
            border: 'none',
            padding: '10px 20px',
            borderRadius: '10px',
            fontSize: '13px',
            fontWeight: '800',
            cursor: 'pointer',
            boxShadow: '0 4px 6px -1px rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.3)'
          }}
        >
          + Registrar Nueva Carga
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Banner de referencia en vivo desde API CNE */}
      <div style={{
        background: 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.08)',
        border: '1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.25)',
        borderRadius: '12px',
        padding: '12px 18px',
        display: 'flex',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        alignItems: 'center',
        flexWrap: 'wrap',
        gap: '12px'
      }}>
        <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: '#cbd5e1' }}>
          âš¡ <strong style={{ color: '#38bdf8' }}>Referencia API CNE ({fuelLabels[refFuelType]}):</strong>{' '}
          {isCneLoading
            ? 'Consultando precios en lÃ­nea...'
            : cheapestStation
              ? (
                <>
                  MÃ¡s econÃ³mica en <strong>{cheapestStation.commune}</strong>: {cheapestStation.name} â€”{' '}
                  <strong style={{ color: '#4ade80' }}>
                    ${Number(cheapestStation[refFuelType]).toLocaleString('es-CL')}/L
                  </strong>
                </>
              )
              : 'Sin precios disponibles'}
        </span>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px' }}>
          {Object.entries(fuelLabels).map(([key, label]) => (
            <button
              key={key}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setRefFuelType(key)}
              style={{
                background: refFuelType === key ? '#38bdf8' : 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.6)',
                color: refFuelType === key ? '#0f172a' : '#94a3b8',
                border: '1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.3)',
                borderRadius: '6px',
                padding: '4px 10px',
                fontSize: '11px',
                fontWeight: '800',
                cursor: 'pointer'
              }}
            >
              {label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Tabla */}
      {fuelRecords.length === 0 ? (
        <div style={{ background: '#1e293b', border: '1px solid #334155', borderRadius: '16px', padding: '40px', textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
          No hay cargas de combustible registradas todavÃ­a.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ background: '#1e293b', border: '1px solid #334155', borderRadius: '16px', overflow: 'hidden', boxShadow: '0 10px 25px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3)' }}>
          <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', textAlign: 'left', fontSize: '14px', color: '#cbd5e1' }}>
            <thead>
              <tr style={{ background: '#0f172a', borderBottom: '1px solid #334155', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '12px', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>
                <th style={{ padding: '16px' }}>Fecha</th>
                <th style={{ padding: '16px' }}>VehÃ­culo</th>
                <th style={{ padding: '16px' }}>OdÃ³metro (Opc.)</th>
                <th style={{ padding: '16px' }}>Litros</th>
                <th style={{ padding: '16px' }}>Costo Total</th>
                <th style={{ padding: '16px', textAlign: 'center' }}>Acciones</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {fuelRecords.map(record => {
                const vId = record.vehicle_id || record.vehicleId;
                const odo = Number(record.odometer || 0);

                return (
                  <tr key={record.id} style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid #334155' }}>
                    <td style={{ padding: '16px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#f8fafc' }}>{record.date}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '16px' }}>{renderVehicleCell(vId)}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '16px', color: odo > 0 ? '#cbd5e1' : '#64748b' }}>
                      {odo > 0 ? `${odo.toLocaleString('es-CL')} km` : 'â€”'}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '16px', fontWeight: '700', color: '#f8fafc' }}>
                      {Number(record.liters || 0).toFixed(1)} L
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '16px', fontWeight: '800', color: '#4ade80' }}>
                      ${Number(record.total || 0).toLocaleString('es-CL')}
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: '16px', textAlign: 'center' }}>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => onDeleteFuel(record.id)}
                        title="Eliminar carga"
                        style={{
                          background: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)',
                          color: '#ef4444',
                          border: '1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.3)',
                          padding: '6px 12px',
                          borderRadius: '8px',
                          fontSize: '12px',
                          fontWeight: '700',
                          cursor: 'pointer'
                        }}
                      >
                        ðŸ—‘ï¸ Eliminar
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}
