==================== ARCHIVO: src\views\MapView.jsx ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: MapView.jsx
  PropÃ³sito: Renderiza un mapa interactivo utilizando Leaflet. Muestra la ubicaciÃ³n 
  de las estaciones de servicio, destaca visualmente la estaciÃ³n mÃ¡s barata cercana 
  segÃºn el filtro de combustible y permite ver detalles al hacer clic en los marcadores.
*/

import React from 'react';
import { MapContainer, TileLayer, Marker, Popup } from 'react-leaflet';
import L from 'leaflet';

// CorrecciÃ³n de Ã­conos por defecto de Leaflet en React
delete L.Icon.Default.prototype._getIconUrl;
L.Icon.Default.mergeOptions({
  iconRetinaUrl: 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/leaflet/1.7.1/images/marker-icon-2x.png',
  iconUrl: 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/leaflet/1.7.1/images/marker-icon.png',
  shadowUrl: 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/leaflet/1.7.1/images/marker-shadow.png',
});

// Coordenadas simuladas o reales para las estaciones (agregando lat/lng a las estaciones de Santiago/Chile)
export default function MapView({ stations, fuelType }) {
  // Coordenadas iniciales centradas en Santiago, Chile
  const positionCenter = [-33.4489, -70.6693];

  // Identificar la estaciÃ³n mÃ¡s barata para el combustible actual
  const validStations = stations.filter(s => s.p95); // Asegurar que tengan precio
  const keyPrice = fuelType === 'diesel' ? 'diesel' : 'p' + fuelType;
  
  let cheapestStation = null;
  if (validStations.length > 0) {
    cheapestStation = validStations.reduce((min, curr) => (curr[keyPrice] < min[keyPrice] ? curr : min), validStations[0]);
  }

  // Generar coordenadas aproximadas basadas en la comuna para el ejemplo visual del mapa
  const getCoordinates = (commune) => {
    const coords = {
      'provi': [-33.432, -70.612],
      'santi': [-33.448, -70.650],
      'uÃ±oa': [-33.456, -70.603],
      'condes': [-33.411, -70.578],
      'macul': [-33.483, -70.598],
      'maipÃº': [-33.510, -70.758],
      'florida': [-33.523, -70.599],
      'bernardo': [-33.593, -70.702],
      'vitacura': [-33.395, -70.574],
      'valpara': [-33.047, -71.612],
      'viÃ±a': [-33.024, -71.551],
      'concep': [-36.826, -73.049],
      'antof': [-23.652, -70.395],
      'temuco': [-38.735, -72.590],
      'puerto': [-41.469, -72.942]
    };
    
    for (let key in coords) {
      if (commune.toLowerCase().includes(key)) return coords[key];
    }
    return [-33.4489 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1, -70.6693 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1]; // Coordenada aleatoria cercana si no coincide
  };

  return (
    <div className="card-box" style={{ padding: '16px', height: '500px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
      <div style={{ marginBottom: '12px', display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: 'bold' }}>ðŸ—ºï¸ Mapa Interactivo de Precios</h3>
        {cheapestStation && (
          <span style={{ background: '#ecfdf5', color: '#059669', padding: '6px 12px', borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 'bold' }}>
            ðŸ”¥ OpciÃ³n mÃ¡s econÃ³mica: {cheapestStation.name} (${cheapestStation[keyPrice]} /L)
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      <div style={{ flex: 1, width: '100%', borderRadius: '12px', overflow: 'hidden', border: '1px solid #e2e8f0' }}>
        <MapContainer center={positionCenter} zoom={12} style={{ height: '100%', width: '100%' }}>
          <TileLayer
            attribution='&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a> contributors'
            url="https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png"
          />
          {stations.map(s => {
            const coords = getCoordinates(s.commune);
            const price = s[keyPrice];
            const isCheapest = cheapestStation && cheapestStation.id === s.id;

            return (
              <Marker key={s.id} position={coords}>
                <Popup>
                  <div style={{ minWidth: '160px' }}>
                    <strong style={{ color: '#0f172a' }}>{s.name}</strong>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b', margin: '2px 0 6px 0' }}>Comuna: {s.commune}</p>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: '14px', color: '#4f46e5', fontWeight: 'bold' }}>
                      ${price ? price.toLocaleString('es-CL') : 'N/D'} <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#94a3b8' }}>/litro</span>
                    </p>
                    {isCheapest && (
                      <span style={{ display: 'block', background: '#059669', color: 'white', textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '10px', padding: '2px', borderRadius: '4px', marginTop: '4px' }}>
                        Â¡La mÃ¡s barata!
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </Popup>
              </Marker>
            );
          })}
        </MapContainer>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


==================== ARCHIVO: src\views\TripView.jsx ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: TripView.jsx
  PropÃ³sito: Cotizador de viajes y servicios de flete con Google Maps (Routes API + Places API)
  usando el rendimiento manual (km/L) del vehÃ­culo o un ajuste directo para el viaje.
*/

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { getManualEfficiency } from '../utils/efficiencyHelper';
import {
  loadGoogleMaps,
  computeStreetRoute,
  searchPlacesChile
} from '../services/googleMapsService';

export default function TripView({ vehicles = [] }) {
  const [selectedVehicleId, setSelectedVehicleId] = useState(vehicles[0]?.id || '');
  const [customKmPerLiter, setCustomKmPerLiter] = useState('');
  const [fuelPrice, setFuelPrice] = useState(1320);
  const [tolls, setTolls] = useState('');
  const [maintenanceReserve, setMaintenanceReserve] = useState(45);
  const [profitMargin, setProfitMargin] = useState(35);
  const [distanceKm, setDistanceKm] = useState('');
  const [durationText, setDurationText] = useState('');
  const [clickStep, setClickStep] = useState(0);
  const [mapError, setMapError] = useState('');
  const [isRouting, setIsRouting] = useState(false);

  const [originQuery, setOriginQuery] = useState('');
  const [destQuery, setDestQuery] = useState('');
  const [originResults, setOriginResults] = useState([]);
  const [destResults, setDestResults] = useState([]);

  const mapRef = useRef(null);
  const mapInstanceRef = useRef(null);
  const markerOriginRef = useRef(null);
  const markerDestRef = useRef(null);
  const polylineRef = useRef(null);
  const originPointRef = useRef(null);
  const destPointRef = useRef(null);
  const skipOriginSearchRef = useRef(false);
  const skipDestSearchRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedVehicleId && vehicles.length > 0) {
      setSelectedVehicleId(vehicles[0].id);
    }
  }, [vehicles, selectedVehicleId]);

  const selectedVehicle = vehicles.find(v => v.id === selectedVehicleId) || vehicles[0];

  // Sincronizar el rendimiento manual del vehÃ­culo seleccionado
  useEffect(() => {
    const manualVal = getManualEfficiency(selectedVehicle);
    setCustomKmPerLiter(manualVal > 0 ? manualVal : 11.5);
  }, [selectedVehicle]);

  const kmPerLiter = Number(customKmPerLiter) > 0 ? Number(customKmPerLiter) : 11.5;

  const drawRouteBetween = async (origin, dest) => {
    const map = mapInstanceRef.current;
    if (!map || !window.google?.maps || !origin || !dest) return;

    setIsRouting(true);
    setMapError('');
    try {
      const routeData = await computeStreetRoute(origin, dest);
      setDistanceKm(routeData.distanceKm);
      setDurationText(routeData.durationText);

      if (polylineRef.current) {
        polylineRef.current.setMap(null);
      }

      const decodedPath = window.google.maps.geometry.encoding.decodePath(routeData.encodedPolyline);
      polylineRef.current = new window.google.maps.Polyline({
        path: decodedPath,
        geodesic: true,
        strokeColor: '#3b82f6',
        strokeOpacity: 0.95,
        strokeWeight: 5,
        map
      });

      const bounds = new window.google.maps.LatLngBounds();
      decodedPath.forEach(pt => bounds.extend(pt));
      map.fitBounds(bounds, 50);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error al calcular ruta:', err);
      setMapError(err.message || 'No se pudo trazar la ruta por calles.');
    } finally {
      setIsRouting(false);
    }
  };

  const setOriginMarker = (latLngObj, labelText = '') => {
    const map = mapInstanceRef.current;
    if (!map || !window.google?.maps) return;

    originPointRef.current = latLngObj;
    if (labelText) {
      skipOriginSearchRef.current = true;
      setOriginQuery(labelText);
    }

    if (markerOriginRef.current) markerOriginRef.current.setMap(null);

    const marker = new window.google.maps.Marker({
      position: latLngObj,
      map,
      draggable: true,
      label: { text: 'A', color: '#ffffff', fontWeight: 'bold' },
      title: 'Origen (arrastrable)'
    });

    marker.addListener('dragend', (ev) => {
      const updated = { lat: ev.latLng.lat(), lng: ev.latLng.lng() };
      originPointRef.current = updated;
      if (destPointRef.current) {
        drawRouteBetween(updated, destPointRef.current);
      }
    });

    markerOriginRef.current = marker;
  };

  const setDestMarker = (latLngObj, labelText = '') => {
    const map = mapInstanceRef.current;
    if (!map || !window.google?.maps) return;

    destPointRef.current = latLngObj;
    if (labelText) {
      skipDestSearchRef.current = true;
      setDestQuery(labelText);
    }

    if (markerDestRef.current) markerDestRef.current.setMap(null);

    const marker = new window.google.maps.Marker({
      position: latLngObj,
      map,
      draggable: true,
      label: { text: 'B', color: '#ffffff', fontWeight: 'bold' },
      title: 'Destino (arrastrable)'
    });

    marker.addListener('dragend', (ev) => {
      const updated = { lat: ev.latLng.lat(), lng: ev.latLng.lng() };
      destPointRef.current = updated;
      if (originPointRef.current) {
        drawRouteBetween(originPointRef.current, updated);
      }
    });

    markerDestRef.current = marker;
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    loadGoogleMaps()
      .then((maps) => {
        if (!isMounted || !mapRef.current || mapInstanceRef.current) return;

        const map = new maps.Map(mapRef.current, {
          center: { lat: -33.4489, lng: -70.6693 },
          zoom: 12,
          streetViewControl: false,
          mapTypeControl: false,
          fullscreenControl: true
        });

        map.addListener('click', (e) => {
          const clicked = { lat: e.latLng.lat(), lng: e.latLng.lng() };

          if (!originPointRef.current || (originPointRef.current && destPointRef.current)) {
            destPointRef.current = null;
            setDistanceKm('');
            setDurationText('');
            skipDestSearchRef.current = true;
            setDestQuery('');
            if (markerDestRef.current) markerDestRef.current.setMap(null);
            if (polylineRef.current) polylineRef.current.setMap(null);

            setOriginMarker(clicked, `${clicked.lat.toFixed(5)}, ${clicked.lng.toFixed(5)}`);
            setClickStep(1);
          } else {
            setDestMarker(clicked, `${clicked.lat.toFixed(5)}, ${clicked.lng.toFixed(5)}`);
            setClickStep(0);
            drawRouteBetween(originPointRef.current, clicked);
          }
        });

        mapInstanceRef.current = map;
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (isMounted) setMapError(err.message);
      });

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (skipOriginSearchRef.current) {
      skipOriginSearchRef.current = false;
      setOriginResults([]);
      return;
    }
    if (!originQuery || originQuery.trim().length < 3) {
      setOriginResults([]);
      return;
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      const places = await searchPlacesChile(originQuery);
      setOriginResults(places);
    }, 350);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [originQuery]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (skipDestSearchRef.current) {
      skipDestSearchRef.current = false;
      setDestResults([]);
      return;
    }
    if (!destQuery || destQuery.trim().length < 3) {
      setDestResults([]);
      return;
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      const places = await searchPlacesChile(destQuery);
      setDestResults(places);
    }, 350);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [destQuery]);

  const handleSelectOriginPlace = (place) => {
    const point = { lat: place.lat, lng: place.lng };
    setOriginResults([]);
    setOriginMarker(point, `${place.name} - ${place.address}`);
    setClickStep(destPointRef.current ? 0 : 1);

    if (mapInstanceRef.current && !destPointRef.current) {
      mapInstanceRef.current.setCenter(point);
      mapInstanceRef.current.setZoom(14);
    }
    if (destPointRef.current) {
      drawRouteBetween(point, destPointRef.current);
    }
  };

  const handleSelectDestPlace = (place) => {
    const point = { lat: place.lat, lng: place.lng };
    setDestResults([]);
    setDestMarker(point, `${place.name} - ${place.address}`);
    setClickStep(0);

    if (mapInstanceRef.current && !originPointRef.current) {
      mapInstanceRef.current.setCenter(point);
      mapInstanceRef.current.setZoom(14);
    }
    if (originPointRef.current) {
      drawRouteBetween(originPointRef.current, point);
    }
  };

  const handleResetPoints = () => {
    originPointRef.current = null;
    destPointRef.current = null;
    skipOriginSearchRef.current = true;
    skipDestSearchRef.current = true;
    setOriginQuery('');
    setDestQuery('');
    setOriginResults([]);
    setDestResults([]);
    setDistanceKm('');
    setDurationText('');
    setMapError('');
    setClickStep(0);

    if (markerOriginRef.current) markerOriginRef.current.setMap(null);
    if (markerDestRef.current) markerDestRef.current.setMap(null);
    if (polylineRef.current) polylineRef.current.setMap(null);

    if (mapInstanceRef.current) {
      mapInstanceRef.current.setCenter({ lat: -33.4489, lng: -70.6693 });
      mapInstanceRef.current.setZoom(12);
    }
  };

  const numericDistance = Number(distanceKm) || 0;
  const numericTolls = Number(tolls) || 0;
  const hasRouteData = numericDistance > 0;

  const litersRequired = hasRouteData ? numericDistance / kmPerLiter : 0;
  const fuelCost = litersRequired * (Number(fuelPrice) || 0);
  const totalMaintenance = numericDistance * (Number(maintenanceReserve) || 0);
  const operationalCost = fuelCost + numericTolls + totalMaintenance;
  const suggestedRate = operationalCost * (1 + (Number(profitMargin) || 0) / 100);

  return (
    <div className="trip-container">
      <div className="trip-header">
        <h2>ðŸ—ºï¸ Cotizador de Viajes y Servicios de Flete</h2>
        <p>Traza la ruta real por calles (Google Maps), ajusta el rendimiento manual del vehÃ­culo y obtÃ©n tu tarifa sugerida.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="trip-grid">
        {/* COLUMNA IZQUIERDA: BUSCADORES, MAPA Y DISTANCIA */}
        <div className="trip-map-card">
          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '10px', marginBottom: '10px' }}>
            <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="ðŸ“ Buscar Origen (ej: Apoquindo 4500)"
                value={originQuery}
                onChange={(e) => setOriginQuery(e.target.value)}
                className="trip-input"
                style={{ width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box', fontSize: '12px' }}
              />
              {originResults.length > 0 && (
                <div style={{
                  position: 'absolute', top: '100%', left: 0, right: 0, zIndex: 50,
                  background: '#0f172a', border: '1px solid #334155', borderRadius: '8px',
                  marginTop: '4px', maxHeight: '180px', overflowY: 'auto', boxShadow: '0 10px 25px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)'
                }}>
                  {originResults.map(place => (
                    <div
                      key={place.id}
                      onClick={() => handleSelectOriginPlace(place)}
                      style={{ padding: '8px 10px', cursor: 'pointer', borderBottom: '1px solid #1e293b', fontSize: '12px' }}
                    >
                      <strong style={{ color: '#f8fafc', display: 'block' }}>{place.name}</strong>
                      <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '11px' }}>{place.address}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="ðŸ Buscar Destino (ej: Plaza MaipÃº)"
                value={destQuery}
                onChange={(e) => setDestQuery(e.target.value)}
                className="trip-input"
                style={{ width: '100%', boxSizing: 'border-box', fontSize: '12px' }}
              />
              {destResults.length > 0 && (
                <div style={{
                  position: 'absolute', top: '100%', left: 0, right: 0, zIndex: 50,
                  background: '#0f172a', border: '1px solid #334155', borderRadius: '8px',
                  marginTop: '4px', maxHeight: '180px', overflowY: 'auto', boxShadow: '0 10px 25px rgba(0,0,0,0.5)'
                }}>
                  {destResults.map(place => (
                    <div
                      key={place.id}
                      onClick={() => handleSelectDestPlace(place)}
                      style={{ padding: '8px 10px', cursor: 'pointer', borderBottom: '1px solid #1e293b', fontSize: '12px' }}
                    >
                      <strong style={{ color: '#f8fafc', display: 'block' }}>{place.name}</strong>
                      <span style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '11px' }}>{place.address}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="map-controls-bar">
            <div className="click-instruction">
              {isRouting
                ? 'ðŸ”„ Trazando ruta por calles...'
                : clickStep === 0
                  ? 'ðŸ“ Haz 1er Clic en el mapa para Origen (A)'
                  : 'ðŸ Haz 2do Clic en el mapa para Destino (B)'}
            </div>
            <button type="button" onClick={handleResetPoints} className="btn-reset-points">
              Reiniciar Puntos
            </button>
          </div>

          {mapError && (
            <div style={{
              background: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)',
              border: '1px solid rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.4)',
              color: '#fca5a5',
              padding: '8px 12px',
              borderRadius: '8px',
              fontSize: '12px',
              marginBottom: '8px'
            }}>
              âš ï¸ {mapError}
            </div>
          )}

          <div ref={mapRef} className="leaflet-map-box" />

          <div className="distance-input-box" style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px', flex: 1 }}>
              <label>Distancia Ruta Real (KM):</label>
              <input 
                type="number" 
                step="0.1"
                min="0"
                placeholder="0.0"
                value={distanceKm} 
                onChange={(e) => {
                  const val = e.target.value;
                  setDistanceKm(val === '' ? '' : Math.max(0, Number(val)));
                }}
                className="trip-input-highlight"
              />
            </div>

            {durationText && (
              <span style={{
                background: 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.15)',
                border: '1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35)',
                color: '#38bdf8',
                padding: '6px 12px',
                borderRadius: '8px',
                fontSize: '12px',
                fontWeight: '800'
              }}>
                â±ï¸ Tiempo est.: {durationText}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* COLUMNA DERECHA: CONFIGURACIÃ“N Y RESULTADOS */}
        <div className="trip-sidebar-box">
          <div className="trip-form-section">
            <h3>ParÃ¡metros de OperaciÃ³n</h3>
            
            <div className="trip-row-inputs">
              <div className="form-group">
                <label>VehÃ­culo de la Flota *</label>
                <select 
                  value={selectedVehicleId} 
                  onChange={(e) => setSelectedVehicleId(e.target.value)}
                  className="trip-select"
                >
                  {vehicles.map(v => {
                    const manualKmL = getManualEfficiency(v);
                    return (
                      <option key={v.id} value={v.id}>
                        {v.name} ({v.plate || 'Sin Patente'}){manualKmL > 0 ? ` - ${manualKmL} km/L` : ''}
                      </option>
                    );
                  })}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="form-group">
                <label>Rendimiento (km/L)</label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  step="0.1"
                  min="1"
                  value={customKmPerLiter}
                  onChange={(e) => setCustomKmPerLiter(e.target.value)}
                  className="trip-input"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="trip-row-inputs">
              <div className="form-group">
                <label>Precio Bencina ($/L)</label>
                <input 
                  type="number" 
                  value={fuelPrice} 
                  onChange={(e) => setFuelPrice(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="trip-input"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="form-group">
                <label>Peajes / TAG ($)</label>
                <input 
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  placeholder="0"
                  value={tolls} 
                  onChange={(e) => setTolls(e.target.value === '' ? '' : Math.max(0, Number(e.target.value)))}
                  className="trip-input"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="trip-row-inputs">
              <div className="form-group">
                <label>Reserva Desgaste ($/km)</label>
                <input 
                  type="number" 
                  value={maintenanceReserve} 
                  onChange={(e) => setMaintenanceReserve(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="trip-input"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="form-group">
                <label>Margen Ganancia (%)</label>
                <input 
                  type="number" 
                  value={profitMargin} 
                  onChange={(e) => setProfitMargin(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="trip-input"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* DESGLOSE DE COSTOS */}
          <div className="trip-results-card">
            <h3>Desglose Financiero</h3>

            {!hasRouteData ? (
              <div style={{
                padding: '20px 14px',
                textAlign: 'center',
                color: '#94a3b8',
                fontSize: '13px',
                lineHeight: '1.5',
                background: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.5)',
                borderRadius: '10px',
                border: '1px dashed #334155'
              }}>
                ðŸ“ Marca el <strong>Origen</strong> y <strong>Destino</strong> en el mapa (o ingresa una distancia en km) para calcular el costo operativo y la tarifa sugerida.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <>
                <div className="result-row">
                  <span>Combustible Requerido:</span>
                  <strong>{litersRequired.toFixed(1)} Litros</strong>
                </div>
                <div className="result-row">
                  <span>Gasto Combustible:</span>
                  <strong>${Math.round(fuelCost).toLocaleString('es-CL')}</strong>
                </div>
                <div className="result-row">
                  <span>Reserva MantenciÃ³n / NeumÃ¡ticos:</span>
                  <strong>${Math.round(totalMaintenance).toLocaleString('es-CL')}</strong>
                </div>
                <div className="result-row">
                  <span>Peajes y TAG:</span>
                  <strong>${numericTolls.toLocaleString('es-CL')}</strong>
                </div>

                <div className="result-total-box">
                  <span>COSTO OPERATIVO REAL:</span>
                  <span className="total-amount">${Math.round(operationalCost).toLocaleString('es-CL')}</span>
                </div>

                <div className="result-suggested-box">
                  <span>TARIFA SUGERIDA DE FLETE:</span>
                  <span className="suggested-amount">${Math.round(suggestedRate).toLocaleString('es-CL')}</span>
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


==================== ARCHIVO: src\views\VehiclesView.jsx ====================
/*
  Nombre del archivo: VehiclesView.jsx
  PropÃ³sito: GestiÃ³n de vehÃ­culos del garaje con rendimiento manual (km/L) editable en cada tarjeta.
*/

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { getManualEfficiency } from '../utils/efficiencyHelper';

export default function VehiclesView({
  vehicles = [],
  deleteVehicle,
  openModal,
  onUpdateManualEfficiency
}) {
  const [editingEffId, setEditingEffId] = useState(null);
  const [draftEffValues, setDraftEffValues] = useState({});
  const [savingId, setSavingId] = useState(null);

  const toggleQuickEditor = (vehicle) => {
    if (editingEffId === vehicle.id) {
      setEditingEffId(null);
      return;
    }
    setDraftEffValues(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [vehicle.id]: vehicle.manual_efficiency > 0 ? vehicle.manual_efficiency : ''
    }));
    setEditingEffId(vehicle.id);
  };

  const handleQuickSave = async (vehicleId) => {
    if (!onUpdateManualEfficiency) return;
    const raw = String(draftEffValues[vehicleId] ?? '').replace(',', '.');
    const parsed = Math.max(0, Number(raw) || 0);
    const normalized = parsed > 0 ? Number(parsed.toFixed(1)) : 0;

    setSavingId(vehicleId);
    await onUpdateManualEfficiency(vehicleId, normalized);
    setSavingId(null);
    setEditingEffId(null);
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '24px', width: '100%', maxWidth: '1200px', margin: '0 auto', padding: '0 24px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}>
      
      {/* Encabezado */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '16px', borderBottom: '1px solid #334155', paddingBottom: '20px' }}>
        <div>
          <h2 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '28px', color: '#f8fafc', fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: '-0.5px' }}>ðŸš˜ GestiÃ³n de VehÃ­culos</h2>
          <p style={{ margin: '4px 0 0 0', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '14px' }}>Administra tu flota y define el rendimiento manual (km/L) de cada vehÃ­culo.</p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => openModal(null)}
          style={{
            background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #3b82f6, #1d4ed8)',
            color: '#ffffff',
            border: 'none',
            padding: '10px 20px',
            borderRadius: '10px',
            fontSize: '13px',
            fontWeight: '800',
            cursor: 'pointer',
            boxShadow: '0 4px 6px -1px rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.3)'
          }}
        >
          + Agregar Nuevo VehÃ­culo
        </button>
      </div>

      <h3 style={{ margin: 0, color: '#cbd5e1', fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: '800', textAlign: 'center' }}>
        VehÃ­culos Registrados ({vehicles.length})
      </h3>

      {/* Grid de Tarjetas */}
      {vehicles.length === 0 ? (
        <div style={{ background: '#1e293b', border: '1px solid #334155', borderRadius: '16px', padding: '40px', textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8' }}>
          No tienes vehÃ­culos registrados en tu garaje.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(340px, 1fr))', gap: '24px' }}>
          {vehicles.map(v => {
            const kmPerLiter = getManualEfficiency(v);
            const isEditingEff = editingEffId === v.id;

            return (
              <div
                key={v.id}
                style={{
                  background: '#1e293b',
                  border: '1px solid #334155',
                  borderRadius: '16px',
                  overflow: 'hidden',
                  boxShadow: '0 10px 25px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3)',
                  display: 'flex',
                  flexDirection: 'column'
                }}
              >
                {/* Cabecera con Foto del VehÃ­culo */}
                <div style={{ position: 'relative', height: '160px', width: '100%', background: '#0f172a' }}>
                  {v.image_url ? (
                    <img
                      src={v.image_url}
                      alt={v.name}
                      onError={(e) => { e.target.style.display = 'none'; }}
                      style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }}
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <div style={{
                      width: '100%', height: '100%',
                      background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #1e293b, #0f172a)',
                      display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center',
                      fontSize: '48px', opacity: 0.5
                    }}>
                      ðŸš—
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div style={{ position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0, width: '100%', height: '100%', background: 'linear-gradient(to bottom, rgba(15,23,42,0.3) 0%, rgba(30,41,59,0.9) 100%)' }} />

                  {/* Patente */}
                  <span style={{
                    position: 'absolute', top: '12px', left: '12px',
                    background: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.85)', color: '#38bdf8',
                    border: '1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.4)',
                    padding: '4px 10px', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '12px',
                    fontWeight: '900', letterSpacing: '1px', backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)'
                  }}>
                    {v.plate || 'SIN PATENTE'}
                  </span>

                  {/* Acciones */}
                  <div style={{ position: 'absolute', top: '12px', right: '12px', display: 'flex', gap: '6px' }}>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => openModal(v)}
                      style={{ background: 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.85)', color: '#ffffff', border: 'none', padding: '5px 10px', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '700', cursor: 'pointer', backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)' }}
                    >
                      Editar
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => deleteVehicle(v.id)}
                      style={{ background: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.85)', color: '#ffffff', border: 'none', padding: '5px 10px', borderRadius: '6px', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: '700', cursor: 'pointer', backdropFilter: 'blur(4px)' }}
                    >
                      Eliminar
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Cuerpo de la Tarjeta */}
                <div style={{ padding: '20px', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px' }}>
                  <div>
                    <h4 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '20px', color: '#f8fafc', fontWeight: '900' }}>{v.name}</h4>
                    <p style={{ margin: '2px 0 0 0', color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: '13px' }}>{v.brand} {v.model} ({v.year})</p>
                  </div>

                  {/* Kilometraje */}
                  <div style={{ background: '#0f172a', padding: '12px', borderRadius: '10px', border: '1px solid #334155', display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8', fontWeight: '700' }}>Kilometraje:</span>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: '15px', color: '#f8fafc', fontWeight: '800' }}>{Number(v.currentKm || 0).toLocaleString('es-CL')} km</span>
                  </div>

                  {/* Rendimiento Manual */}
                  <div style={{ background: '#0f172a', padding: '12px', borderRadius: '10px', border: '1px solid #334155', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#94a3b8', fontWeight: '700' }}>Rendimiento:</span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: '15px', color: kmPerLiter > 0 ? '#4ade80' : '#94a3b8', fontWeight: '900' }}>
                        {kmPerLiter > 0 ? `${kmPerLiter} km/L` : 'No definido'}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                      <span style={{
                        fontSize: '11px',
                        fontWeight: '700',
                        padding: '3px 8px',
                        borderRadius: '6px',
                        background: kmPerLiter > 0 ? 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.15)' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.12)',
                        border: `1px solid ${kmPerLiter > 0 ? 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35)' : 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.25)'}`,
                        color: kmPerLiter > 0 ? '#38bdf8' : '#94a3b8'
                      }}>
                        {kmPerLiter > 0 ? 'Ingreso Manual' : 'Pendiente de ingreso'}
                      </span>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => toggleQuickEditor(v)}
                        style={{
                          background: 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.12)',
                          color: '#38bdf8',
                          border: '1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.3)',
                          padding: '4px 10px',
                          borderRadius: '6px',
                          fontSize: '11px',
                          fontWeight: '700',
                          cursor: 'pointer'
                        }}
                      >
                        {isEditingEff ? 'Cerrar â–²' : 'âœï¸ Ajustar km/L'}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Editor rÃ¡pido inline */}
                  {isEditingEff && (
                    <div style={{
                      background: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.95)',
                      border: '1px solid rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.4)',
                      borderRadius: '10px',
                      padding: '12px',
                      display: 'flex',
                      flexDirection: 'column',
                      gap: '8px'
                    }}>
                      <label style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#cbd5e1', fontWeight: '700' }}>
                        Rendimiento promedio (km/L):
                      </label>
                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          step="0.1"
                          min="0"
                          placeholder="Ej: 13.5"
                          value={draftEffValues[v.id] ?? ''}
                          onChange={(e) => setDraftEffValues(prev => ({ ...prev, [v.id]: e.target.value }))}
                          style={{
                            flex: 1,
                            background: '#1e293b',
                            border: '1px solid #475569',
                            borderRadius: '8px',
                            padding: '8px 10px',
                            color: '#f8fafc',
                            fontSize: '14px',
                            fontWeight: '800'
                          }}
                        />
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          disabled={savingId === v.id}
                          onClick={() => handleQuickSave(v.id)}
                          style={{
                            background: '#22c55e',
                            color: '#0f172a',
                            border: 'none',
                            borderRadius: '8px',
                            padding: '8px 14px',
                            fontSize: '12px',
                            fontWeight: '800',
                            cursor: 'pointer',
                            opacity: savingId === v.id ? 0.6 : 1
                          }}
                        >
                          {savingId === v.id ? '...' : 'Guardar'}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}
